TALATCHENGANI 
lana skang anga Kii sikni bidingo an tangni seanirangko 
iningchikking ra chimonge “Ku sikni Bidingo Seanirang” ine katta 
bichong on e ki-tap nakatataha. lano Ku sikni bidingo ong gija, 
Literature ba Sea-Jotani bidingo an tangni seanirangko mingchikking 
ra dime, “Sea-Jotani Bidingo Seanirang’’ ine katta bichong on e 
chappa ka attaienga. 
lano man-chapatgipa seanirang mingprakni giniin kan dik 
kan dik aganna ska: (i) “Sea-Jotanio Uie Ra-na Nanggipa Kattarang” 
essayo, Englisho sea-jotanio jakkalronggipa, indiba A chikkuode 
a gittalgipa kattarangni bidingo talataniko daka. (2) Sea bewal- 
rokomko nitoatna aro knatoatan baksana nikjagringatna, aganani- 
seani bimangrangko senokgiparang jakkalronga. Gnigipa essay 
“Aganani Bimangrang”-o indakgipa Aganani Bimangrangko talata. 
(3) Poedo seaniosan ong aigija gipin rokomni seanirangoba tosiisae 
agananirangko jakkalbca, maina iaranga seaniko wasaatan baksana, 
nilgagringatachi talbatatna dakchaka. “Achikkii PoedorangoTosusae 
Agananirang”-o A chikku poedorango jakkalgipa tosusae 
agananirangni bidingosan seaia. la tosusae agananirangna rokomari 
rakgipa kattarang donga. Indiba iia kattarangko iano jakkalja, maina 
ia seaniko ramram gadango uie ra pana sikgiparangnasa miksonga. 
(4) An ching A chikrang me chik poedo segipakode Jonmoni D. 
Shirakosan uitokpaaia, Indiba gipin gipin poedo sena changgipa 
A chik me-chikrang gnang. Uamangni poedorang Jonmoni D. Shirani 
seani rokom-bewal aro gadangona sokjanaba donga, indiba uamangni 
seanirangkoba nikehangskanade man ja. la scanio uamangni 
on gilaniko ra chaka aro niksengata. lamangni ja mano poedo 
segiparangko iano man chapja. (5) “A chikku Romantic Poetry”, ian 
ruuta bilsirangna skang segipasa. la seanio ‘romantic poetiy’-ni gimin 
on tisa talatchenge, ia dolo ga akgipa A chikku poedorangko nia. (6) 
“Kan dikgipa Golporang” essayo, mai rokom seaniko short story ba

Kan dikgipa Golpo ine sea-jotanio minga, uko talatna jotton ka a. 
Golpoko kan dike seaiode, uno Kan dikgipa Golponi gunrang 
dongjaode, uko Kan dikgipa Golpo ine aganna man ja. Kan-dikgipa 
Golponi gunrangko ia seanio talata. (7) Snigipa essayara “Kabe”. Kabe- 
ni mangsongani, manggrapani rokotn-bewal, katta bichong aro 
uarang gitako ia essayo nia. (8) ‘‘Dakmesokani’’- ian chetgipa essay. 
“Drama” seani bidingo uie ra-na nanggnirangko iano talata. (9) 
“Dakmesokaniko Senokgipani Gunrang” essayo, Dakmesokaniko 
chu-sokgipa ong-atna maironga changa-sapanirangko nanga aro mai 
chol-bewalrangko senokgiparang jakkala, uarangko talata. (10) 
Bon-kamgipa essay “Chap Dilmong Ge-sangipa aro Chasong Gitalni 
Dakmesokanirang”-o One-Act Playni gunrang aro Chasong Gitalni 
Dakmesokanirangko, A-chikkuo segimin Dakmesokanirangko 
ra*chape, talataniko daka. 
Sea-jotani bidingo skie-poraie ra-enggiparangsan ong-aigija, 
gimikan ku-tango sea-jotani bidingo uie-ma-sie ra-na nanga. Sea- 
jotani, segimin ki-taprang, chasongni chasongna aganrikrikachi tange 
rakkibaenggipa A-chikrangni ring-ani-minganirang, Katta 
Agananirang A-chik mandeko mandeskaming kabinge dona. Uni 
gimin ning-tue apale ong-jaoba, A-chikkuo sea-jotani bidingo 
kosakgilgilde an-ching pilakan uie ra-na nangchongmota. 
Ia seanirangko dakmitingo anga A-chik Poedorang, A-chik 
Dakmesokanirang, MUton S. Sangmani “History of Garo Literature” 
aro “History and Culture of the Garos”, B.Datta, N.C. Sharma aro 
P.C. Dasni “A Handbook of Folklore Material of North East”, Mihir 
N. Sangmani “Maniani Bidik” aro “Pagitchamni Ku-bisring”, Aldrich 
Ch. Mominni “A-chikni Ku-andik” aro Harendra W. Marakni “A-chik 
Aganbewalrang”-ko nirongaha aro jakkalaha. larang baksana, angni 
jakkalgipa gipin ki-taprangara Michael Meyerni “The Bedford 
Introduction To Literature” (Second Edition), Lee A. Jacobusni “The 
Bedford Introduction To Drama” aro William Henry Hudsotmi “An 
Introduction To The Study of Literature”. 
“Kabe”-ni gimin semitingo anga talbate ra-na gita, Miksakgre 
songchi re-ange Pa Agat A. Sangmako grongea aro ua angna aditakon 
taldapate on-aha.

lako semitingo, anga angni Cotton Collegeo English Honours ra-e 
poraiako gisik ra-ata. Angni porairimska saksa, Indiani 
senokgiparangni bidingo aganchapmitingo, Shri Rabindranath 
Tagore, ine janapa. Chingni Skigipa ka-dingnapbeaha. “Mr. 
Shakespeare, Mr. Wordsworth, Mrs. Elizabeth Browning ine seako 
na-a banoba nikama? lamangko mandena mande raja, uamangni 
namnambegipa seanirangnasa. Manderangsiahaoba, uamangko Late 
Shakespeare ine seja, maina uamangni seanirang pangkama aro 
janggi tangkama. Uamangni seanirangko talatmitingode, man*a dipet 
Present Tenseko (Da-o Ong-gipa Tense) jakkalna nanga. Ian 
Literatureni rokom-bewal”, ine ua talataha. Uni kattaran^o gisik ra>e, 
anga indinari janapanio Pa, Ma inaniko jakkaloba, senokgiparangni 
kamrangni bidingo talatmitingode, Da-o Ong-gipa Tenseko jakkale 
sena jotton ka-a. Uni gimin iano Pa, Ma ine segijaniara mande 
ra-gijaniko mesokani ong-ja; sea-jotani pangkamaniko mesoknasa 
ong-a. 
Pa Keneth M. Momin A-chikku sea-jotanina gisik 
nangchongmotgipa ong-a. Ua adita ki-taprangko see uarangko chappa 
ka-ataha. Uni gimin ia angni ki-tap “Sea-Jotani Bidingo Seanirang”- 
na uan ‘‘Agansoani”-ko sena namgen ine, anga Pa Mominko, angko 
dakchakpachina phoneosa mol-molataiaha. Angni seanina 
‘‘Agansoani”-ko sena phonechisa mol-molahaomangba, ua 
jechakgijan uko seaha. Agansoaniko see angna didiataniko aro angni 
ki-tapna mikkimko on-anina anga Pa Keneth M. Mominko mittelbea. 
Dated, Tura,

AGANPAANI KATTARANG 
A-chik ku-siko sea-jotanirang, gipin jatrang baksa tosusaode 
namen komibe-engkua ine da-alona kingkingba aganna man-a. Una 
bateba A-chikni sea-jotani bidingo sandie, chanchibewale seanirangde 
namen komibatkuenga. Uni giminan anga an-tangde basakon A-chikni 
sea-jotani kamrang bang-rorognok aro namnamgipa, gamchatgipa 
ki-taprangko A-chik chanchibewalrakgipa manderang nakatatgnok,

gisiktango nisengsoe dongengachim. Segiminrangni gimin ning-tue 
chanchie, seani bewalrangni gimin u-i-ma-sie, segiparangni 
chanchiani bidingrangni gimin chanchibewalskae uamangni 
miksongani, gisikna mesokania maikai rokom rokom 
chanchianirangko, ma-sianirangko manderangni ka-tongo nabaata 
indakgipa segimin ki-tapna anga gisiktango ma-ringe dongengachim. 
Unon pringsao Ma. B. K Sangma an-tangni segimin ki-tapna “Sea- 
jotani Fidingo Seanirang”-na talatchenganiko see on-pachina tele- 
phone l:a-e ango mol-molaha aro uni segiminko ki-tap dakna 
tarigiminko angona watataha. 
Uni segimin ki-tapko poraie nion angni sintie, nichaksoe 
dongenganina Nokgipa angna aganchakaha gita anga 
chanchimanpila. Gipin segimin A-chikni sea-jotani bidingo 
segiminrangna bate Ma. B. K. Sangma namen A-chik ku-siko segimin 
aro chapa ka-gimin ki-taprangko sandirikkote seaniko anga 
chagrongaha. Ma. B. K. Sangma an-tangan English Literatureko skie 
ra-gipa aro Tura Government Collegeo bang-a bQsirangna side on-gipa 
ong-e bang-a man-dapanirang aro an-tangni chimonganirangko 
taridape nambegipa ki-tapko dakna cholko ra-aha. 
la ki-tapo A-chikkuo segimin bang-a chanchibewale seanirang, 
namnamgipa poedorang (Ku-minchi), dakmesokanirang, Donmitape 
seanirang aro ring-ringanirang, ma-ringanirangni gimin Ma. B. K. 
Sangmani sandianiko aro ma-sisokaniko aro una agreba gital gital 
nikanirangko aro chanchianirangko niksoe anga an-tangba bang-a 
ma-sidapanirangko man-aha. 
Ma. B. K. Sangma indakgipa ki-tapko sena ru-uta bilsirangko ra-e 
chanchibewalna, chanchirikkotna aro uarangko sea-jotani bimangona 
iv 
ra-bana gita neng-bee kam ka^aha ine ia ki-tapan sakki on-a. Uni sandie 
nikgiminrangko seskaaniko poraie nie anga aiao inmanpila. 
A-ningo donggipa gamrangni gimin ramram 
songdongpaaigiparangde maiko u-ipaa? Indiba uarangko 
sandigiparangsa, gisik a-ning bite ma-sigiparangsa banoniko maikai 
am-na nanga uani gimin tala. Gamchatbegipa ro-ongrang bano donga 
aro uarangko maikai am-e nikna man-gen uamangsa u-ia. Indiba

basakoba atchu-ambini tangka gope dongipako, darangni u-igijao 
donggenchimoba, maibakai a-a cho-mitingo chagaka gita aro uko 
nikgipana bang-a pattianiko on-a gita A-chikni sea-jotani bidingo 
bang-bea gitalgipa gamrangko, mingsinggipa ro-ongrangko, gisikni 
gamna ia ki-tapo nikna man-a. 
la ki-tapo Ma. B. K. Sangma an-chingni katta jakkalanirang, 
ku-minchi ong-chong, -ku-akma ong-chong ba je rokom seanichiba 
chanchianiko parakatani ong-chong, uarangni rokom aro bewalko 
sandirikkite uarangni bilsuanirangko an-chingna parake on-aha. 
Indakgipa ki-tapkosa A-chik poraigiparangna, mongsongbate 
College aro Universityo poraienggiparangna, namen nangbeenga. Uni 
gimin ia ki-tap jakkaltobegipasan ong-aijawa, indiba indake 
sandirikkite, chanchibewale, gital gital nika-nianirangko niknakgipa 
sakgipin manderangna dilaniko on-gen ine anga ka-dongchakbea. 
la ki-tapko A-chik jinmarangba poraie nie bang-bea u-idapani- 
rangko man-gen aro gisikni apaldapaniko ong-atgen ine anga 
ka-donga. 
Anga ia ki-tapna ‘Talatchenganiko” an-tangko kranikjaha. Uni 
gimin ia ki-tapko mande ra-na “Aganpaani Kattarang”-kosa sena anga 
namnikaha. 
Ma. B. K. Sangma indakgipa namgipa, gamcbatgipa seanirangko 
mikkangchiba ong-katatkugen ine anga ka-donga aro indakachin jatna 
dal-bea dakchakaniko on-gilpagen.

SEA-JOTANIO UIE RA-NA 
NANGGIPA KATTARANG 
Mandeni sena-jotna changaniko uni katta jakkalani, sentence 
rikani, tosusaachi talatani, nilgagringatani, Aganani Bimangrangko 
ba figures of speechko jakkalani aro uarang gitao paraka. Name katta 
jakkalani aro sentence rikaniara seaniko aro ku agananiko knatoata. 
Aganani Bimangrangko krae jakkalaniara agananio aro seanio orto 
ning'tubatata, gajetdapata aro mesubatata. Sea-jotanio ga*kime bak 
ra pagijagiparangba, ia kattarangni aro bewalrangni bidingo uie 
ra pana skaia aro uie ra*anio maming namgijani ba gimaani gri. la 
seanina bang gija kattarangkosan seokaiaha. English Literature aro 
Languageo jakkalgipa English kattarangkon iano jakkalaia aro bano 
man a uno talatdapaniko on a. Da-ode kattarangko nina. 
RHETORIC : Rhetoric katta Greek katta “rhetor”-oni ong kata, 
jeni miksonganian “jinmana aganprakgipa”. Rhetoric kattani maade 
Noun. Dictionaiyo uni ortoko indake on-a: “Art of using impressively 
in speech and writing; language with much display and 
ornamentation, often with the implication of insincerity and 
exaggeration.’* lani miksonganiara, agananio aro seanio kattarangko 
gisik- ka-tongni re-anirango nangatpile jakkalna changa- sapani. 
An-tangni changa- sapako parakna ba mesokna ske, kattarangko 
taridape, nitodapate, talatdape mitamde sea. Indake dakaniara 
basakobade uiatango changatango gaora dee seani bewalba ong-pila 
aro ia bewalara seenggipani ba aganenggipani gisik konggrangani, 
kakketgijani aro dugapile agananiko parakaniona sokata. Mitamrang 
indonga bewalko namnika; mitamrangara nangana bate katta 
jakkalaniko mikchepskaa. 
Skangsrangode, rhetoric inon, jinmana aganprakgipani 
knatimgiparangko gisik ka-tong nom-ate, gisik ka-tong moatpile

jingjengatpile aganna changa-sapanikosa miksonga. Indiba da-o 
aganna changanina agreba seanio katta taridape nitodapate seani 
bewalkoba man-chapa. Indaka bewalko jakkalna changgipa-sapgipako 
rhetorician ine agana. 
RHETORICAL: Dictionaiyo ia kattako indine talata: “using a 
style designed to impress or persuade, artificial or exaggerated in 
1 
language.” Mitam agangiparang ba segiparang namsangsang aganaija 
ba seaija. Katta mingsa minggnichi talatna man aigipako uamang 
talatdapdape ro-ding ro-dap agana ba sea. Uamang an-tangtangko 
katta man-gipa, aganna ba sena changgipa ine mesokna ska. Un 
baksanaba, knatimgiparangko ba seako poraigiparangko gisik moatna 
ba jingjengatnaba ia bewalko mitam):ang jakkala. Indiba 
ong-rongbewaloni re-badeode ba dugapilode katta jrimdugaani 
giminan biritchu bidual gimapila aro seani miksonganikon 
nikgualatpila. Knatimgiparangba ajampila, seako poraigiparangba 
mikrimangpila. 
RHETORICAL QUESTION; la katta tokni miksonganiko 
English'O indake on-a: “Question asked for the sake of effect, to 
impress people, no answer being needed or expected.” lako talatna 
anga angni “Manden’ Bichal” poedoni bon-kamgipa ritinggniko ra-a: 
Sawasa talatgen 
Janggia sanisa? 
la ritingrango on-gipa sing-anina aganchakaniko on-na nangani 
dongja. lako segipa an-tangba aganchakaniko man-nade sing-ja; uko 
man-na ka-dongchakaniba uo dongja. Manderangko ua kattana gisik 
nangatnasan rhetorical questionko on-aia. Indake sing-anichi 
knatimgipani ba seako poraigipani gisikko su-dikna, 
nangchongmotgipa bakona gisik salangpilna aro uni bidingo ning-tue 
chanchiatna man-ani gimin, ian seanio nambegipa chol mingsa ong-a. 
PROSODY : Prosody katta Greek katta “prosodia”-oniong-kata, 
jeni ortoan somoe melie, tok tok dake ring-gipa git. Dictionaryo 
prosodyni miksonganiko indake on-a: “Science of verse rhythms or 
metres; rh)rthm, pause, tempo, stress and pitch (of a language).” 
Ra-gopede, ian poedo seani bewal aro niamrangko miksonga. Poedo

seanio podrangko maidake sea aro dona, ritingrangko badita dona, 
maidake meli-ma-gapata, katta-tongrang ritingprako badita ge- 
donga, ritingantini bon-kamgipa katta-tongrang ma-rapama, 
ma-rapaniara ritinggni ma-atama ba deda dedasa, ia pilakkon 
prosodyni niamrangni ning-o tik ong-ata. 
“Science” inahaon jebasi bingbang seaiani ong-jajok. Seani 
niamrangko chon-toke ong-jaoba bang-batakon ja-rikna nangaijok. 
Beben chasong gitalo poedo segiparang poedo seani gital ja-kolko 
I sanditokengjok aro indaken uamang an-tangtangni seanirango 
' donsogimin niamrangko kingkot ja-rilgajok. Da-ororoni seanirang 
“free verse” ba donsogimin niamrango ga-chatgija jakgitele segipa 
poedorang ong-bata. 
RHYTHM : Rhythm katta Greek katta rhuthmos-oni ong*kata 
aro rhuthmos-in rheo kattaoni ong-katskaa. Rheoni ortoara chi gita 
joka. Ku-sikni ba ku-aganani somoe melie, tiktak ong-e, chi joka gita 
name re-aniko aro git gita knatoe sulsul gam-aniko rhythm ine agana. 
Rhythmko “regular succession of weak and strong stresses, accents, 
sounds or movements in speech, music and dancing,” ine agana. 
Ku-agananio, kattani-tongrangko badiabarango ga-jete, 
badiabarangko ga-jetgija, jerangkon ‘strong’ aro ‘weak’ stresses ine 
agana, ta-rak ta-rak saljole agana. Kattani gam-anirang aro 
re-anirangba tik ong-a, sulsulba ong-a. Kattarangko jeoba tong dake, 
jeoba skatang ga-jete, uarangko jebasi aganaiode, ua agananio rhythm 
dongja, katta agana knatoja ine an-ching agana. Dondikna 
nanganirango dondikgija aganani, poraiani aro seaniara ku-sikni 
tiktak ong-e re-aniko peng-panga aro indake ku-sik jakkalanio 
knatoani dongja. Rochona seani bimangoba ong-bo poedoni 
bimangoba ong-bo, ga-jetna nangao ga-jete, dondikna nangao dondike 
aganosa ku-aganani me-sua. 
METRE : Greek katta metron-oni metre katta ong-kata aro 
metronni ortode “toani”. Dictionaryo metre-ni ortoko indine on-a: 
“Verse rhythm; fixed arrangement of accented and unaccented 
syllables.” Kattarangko katta-tongrangcbi ong-ata, jerangoniko 
mitamko ga-jete minga ba agana aro mitamko ga-jetgija joljol ramram 
dake min g aia . la ga-jetgipa katta-tongrangko tok tok done, tik tik dake,

apsan apsan toe, toksao badita katta tongrang gnang, ua gitan gipin 
tokrangoba donaniko metre ine agana. Poedorangosa iandake 
ong-bataia. Ku-akmao seode, dondikanirang krana aro tik ong-na 
nanga, indiba dondikani chin minggnini gisepo apsan katta-tongrang 
aro ga-jete aro ga-jetgija agangipa katta-tongrang apsan dongna nanga 
ine niam donsoa dongja. 
Ka-mao on-gipa ritinggniko Jonmoni D. Shirani “Basako” 
poedooniko ra-a: 
3 
“Minggenma antangko atchijok rasongo, 
Atchie indaka nampaja biapo”. 
la ritingrangko niode, mingtoa tokprako katta-tong ge-gittam 
donga ine nikgen. Jekai: 
Ming-'gen-ma / an-'tang-ko / at-'chi-jok / ra-'song-o 
At- 'chi-e / in-'dak-a / nam-'pa-ja / bi-'ap-o. 
Skanggipa ritingko nina. Ritingni kattarangko tokbri dake 
mingosa mingtoa aro uni giminan uno tokbrisan dongna man-aia. 
Tokprako katta-tong ge-gittam gittam donga. lano tokprakni jatchio 
donggipa katta-tong ga-jetako man-gipa ong-a aro ga-jeta man-gipa 
katta-tongko ia (') chinchi mesoka. Tokprakni ja-mantap kan-dike 
salgroe tokprakni ma-ataniko mesoka. Kosako on-gimin gita 
ritingrangko mingon mingtoa aro mingo sul re-a baksa knatoaniba 
gnang. 
Apsan ritingkon tok sualaniko dingtangate niskana. 
Ming-gen-ma an-tang-ko / at-chi-jok ra-song-o. 
Tokprakko ma-atani chinko nikahaosa dondike, ritingko minge 
nibo. Chong-motan, mingmitingo “an-tangko” inani ja-manosa 
kan-dikbee neng-takna man-gen. Minge nion dingtanganiko nikgen. 
Tokgni dake mingode knatoja; tokbri dake mingosa ritingni re-ani 
somoe tik ong-a aro mingoba knatoa. la somoe re-aniko rakkia. 
Basakoba badiaba ritingo katta-tong ge-sa gitchaode, uko ta-rake 
ba seng-sote minge somoeni re-aniko meliata. Basakobara, badiaba 
toko katta tong badeode, tokantio katta-tong badita ge- donga, uming 
meliatna gita, katta-tong ge-sako ra-gale kattako kan-dikata. Jekai, 
“nang-ni” ine katta chu-gimik segija, “-ni” katta-tongko ra-gale “nang”

ine sea. 
STRESS : "Stress is a result of extra force, used in speaking, on 
a particular word or syllable,” ine Dictionaryo kattani miksonganiko 
on-a. Mande pangnande aganrongbewalo aganja. Aganenggipani 
gisikni re-ani kri uni aganani bewalba dingtangaia. Je Imttakoba ra-e, 
aganrongbewal agane nibo aro uni ja-mano ka-ananga gnang apsan 
kattakon agantaie ba mingtaie nibo. "Pangnan” kattako ra-e nina. 
4 
^ “Pangnan” kattao katta-tong ge-gni donga aro ge-gnian ga-jetgipa 
: katta-tongrang ong-ja, ge-gnikon apsan bU on-gija mingaia ba aganaia. 
“Pang 'nan na-a indake dakronga” ine ka-anange aganode an-ching 
gnigipa katta -tongko gagete agana. Skanggipa katta-tong “pang*" ko 
ga-jete minge nibo. Agangipa minggipa an-tangan uko kra-melie 
nilgawa aro knatimgipanaba knadikbeani ong-a. Skanggipa katta- 
tongko ga-jete gam-atna man-dika aro neng-a. landakgipa aganani 
bewalrang aganani gunko dingtangata aro aganani miksonganikoba 
jujaata. A-chikkuo indin aganaiode, stress jakkalani dongja; gisikni 
re-ani dingtangosa, word stressni katta nakata. 
English ku-sikode katta-tong ge-gni donggipa kattaode stress ba 
ga-jete aganani dongaijok. lani bidingo seng-sote uina gita, English 
katta “present”-ko ra-na. “Present" kattako Noun, Adjective aro Verb 
gita jakkalna man-a Stress dongode, ia (') chinko ga-jete aganna ba 
gam-atna nanggipa katta-tongni mikkango on-a. “Present" kattako 
Noun ba Adjective gita jakkalode, stress skanggipa katta-tongo ga-aka. 
Indake stressni chinko on-a: 'present. Indiba uko Verb gitasa 
jakkalode, stress gnigipa katta-tongosa ga-aka. Unon gnigipa katta- 
tongni mikkangosa chin on-e, “pre'sent” ine sea. Present kattako 
dingtang dingtang kattani ma-ao donon, spelling apsan ong-aioba, 
vowelrangni gam-ani gunrang dingtangaijok aro uarang dingtang 
dakesa gam-aijok. 
Englishode, kattani stress dingtangjajok. Apsan katta, apsan 
kattani ma-a aro apsan bimang ong-ode, stress biap jilja. Name 
ka-sariri aganoba ka-anange aganoba, stress pangnan apsan kattani- 
tongon ga-akaia. A-chikkuode ramram aganmitingo katta-tongrangko 
ga-jetja ba uarango stress dongja. Indiba kattako ga-jete aganani ba

word-stressde A-chikkuoba donga. Basakoba “raka" (-) -kon stress 
ba accent ine ma-sisrettoka. Badiaba A-chikku kattarangko tik ong-e 
gam-atna gita, ku-aganmitingo balwa ong-katbagipako gitoko bik dake 
champengrika. Stress aro accentnade indake balwa champengrikani 
gri. “Cha-a" kattako gam-aton, skanggipa katta-tong “cha" ko 
gam-atani ja-mantapan balwa watako champenga aro ia 
champengrikaniko watpilesa ba olgrokatpilesa, gnigipa katta-tong 
“-a” ko minga ba gam-ata. Ia gitoko balwa champenganiko mesokna 
gita A-chikkuo glottal stop ba “raka"-ko on-a aro una ia (-) chinko 
jakkala.

ACCENT : Accentko “prominence given to a syllable by means 
of stress or intonation” ine Dictionaryo orto sea. Git ring-anio katta 
tokko mingna somoe ra-ani aro sulan mongsonggipa ong-a. Gitni sulko 
accent-an namata. Accent inon badiaba katta-tongrangko bU bate ba 
ku-rang sale jakkalaniko miksonga. Ku-agananioba apsan ong-a. 
Nangani kri, gisikni re-ani kri aro jolni kri badiaba kattarangni katta- 
tongrangko ga-jete agana aro sul sale ku-rang saleba agana. Indake 
aganani bewalonikosa an-ching sawa badia jolni mande uisotna 
man-a. 
Mande dolprakni, jolprakni agananio dingtangmancha aganani 
gunrang ba bewalrang donga. Mandeni ku-agananio gam-atani bewal 
aro sul salania badia jolni mandekosan uiataia ong-ja; songsaro 
chugipa gadangnima ongipa gadangni, lekka-pora bida budi donggipa 
nokdangnima ramram nokdangni, iarang pilakkon paraka. la aganani 
rokom aro bewalrangko man-gopesa “accent” ine agana. Mandeko 
niari badia jolni mande uijaoba, uni aganani bewalko uni ku-sik oarin 
uiaia aro uan uni jolkoba mesokaia. A-chikrangni jinmana ra-gatgimin 
A-we ku-sikko aganomangba joltangni accentkode watna man-ja; 
Atongma, Rugama, A-bengma uiaia. 
Ku-agananiara aiao inmanpilgipa mandeni bewal ong-a. Mandeko 
uina gita, mandemanchako nikjaoba ong-aia. Aganenggipako 
mandechongchong nikjaoba, uni ku-rangko knaode, uni aganani 
bewal aro ku-rang salaonikon uara amikkasa askisa ine uisona 
man-aia.Mandeni re-a doani bewal, ua somoemitingo uni be-enni

obosta aro gisikni re-aniko uinaba, mandemanchako nikna nangja. 
Saani, saknaani, bilgriani, ka-saani, ka-sachakani aro ka-anangani 
ku-rang dingtangaia. Kattamanchako knajaoba, ku-rangko knaarin, 
mandeni be-en aro gisikni obostako uisona man-aia. Ian accentni 
gimin indake ong-a. 
PITCH : “Pitch” kattako ring-anio ba gito aro agananioba jakkala. 
“Pitch in music or speech is the degree of highness or lowness,” ine ia 
kattani ortoko Dictionaryo on-a. Mande teng-krange ba chue aro 
imbike ba one git ring-a ba ku-agana. landake ku-rang teng-kranganiko 
aro imbQcaniko ba ku-rang gam-ani rokomko dingtangate agana nie hi 
ku-agananiko knatoata, gipinni gisikko salna man-a aro knatimna 
arataniko nakatatja. 
6 
Ku-rang teng>kranga inoba, badita teng-kranga, imbika inoba 
badita imbika uni gadang donga. la gadang dingtanggrikanian pitch. 
INTONATION: Dictionaiyo on-ani gitade, “Intonation is the 
rise and fall of pitch of the voice in speaking; this is an element of 
meaning in language.” Ku-agananio ku-rang saldoa aro salonaniba 
donga aro uni kri ku-aganani sulba dingtanga. Aganenggipa mande 
gisiktango sukni ba dukni, namnikani ba namnikgijani aro indakgipa 
gisik-ka-tongni re-aniko mikkang bimango mesokjanaba donga. 
Knatimenggipa uni ku-rang salanionikosa uko rim-chipe uina 
man-aia. Mikkango ku-chilo ka-dingsmita gnangsa agangenoba, gisiko 
ka-anangani ba chonnikani dongode, aganani orto manchako, uni sul 
sale kattako ku-rang saldoa salona dake agananionikosa uibata. Uni 
gimin kattani miksongani manchana agreba, kattani orto dongkua. 
TEMPO: Git ring-ani aro ku-agananioba ta-raka ta-rakgijani 
donga. la ta-rakani gadangko tempo ine agana. Git ring-o an-ching 
time (somoe) doka ine aganronga. la somoe rakkianiko 
nangchongmota. Jinma ring-anirango saoba ta-rake ring-ango, 
saobade ka-snesa kattarangko minge ring-ode, gitan bQding bildang 
ong-srangaignok aro katchaatani pal knatimna sikgijanikosa 
nakatatskagen. 
PAUSE: Pauseko “ short interval or stop while singing or saying 
something”, ine Dictionaryo orto on-a. A-chikkuode ring-mitingo ba

ku-aganmitingo dondikani ine donna man-a. Dondikgija 
ring-joljolaina ba ku-aganjoljolnade man-ja, maina balwa opna aro 
watnade dondikna nangtelaia. Una agreba, dondik-donchanggija 
ring-ode ba ku-aganode maimanchako aganenga ba ring-enga ukon 
ma-sijawa; ma-sijaode an-ching knatimna aratnika; knatoaniba uno 
dongjawa. 
Ring-ao aro ku-aganao balwa namen mongsonggipa kamko ka-a. 
Rang-siton namgipa balwako ope, an-chi rongtalatna jakkalgimin 
balwako rang-sitgala. Cbanggni rang-sitani gisepgriko baditaba katta 
mingrangkosan ring-na ba aganna man-aia. Balwa gital opnade 
neng-takna ba dondikna nangtelaia. la indake dondikanikon pause 
ine agana. 
Dondikaniko mesokna adita chinrang donga. Kan-dikbeesan 
7 
dondikna nangaigipana commako (,) aro on-tisa una bate dondikna 
nanggipana semi-colonko (;) jakkala. Aganna nanggipa tokko 
matchotahaode period ba full-stopko (.) jakkala. Garo Reader Ill-ni 
“Alabok aro Chalakkigipa Ang-ke” lessononiko mesokani mingsako 
ka-mao on-a: 
“lanoni adita chel-ao donggipa, dal-begipa aro tu-begipa chibolo 
donena man-gen; maina uano kenchakani gri”. 
Basakobade, aganenggipa kattarang bang-jaoba aro don'dikgija 
aditan aganna man-genchimoba, agananio dondikani chinrangko 
jakkala. Apsan ma-ani kattarangko baditaba mingko joljol mingna 
ba sena nangode, mingantiko ma-ekatna gita commako jakkala. Un 
baksana, aganenggipa mande badiaba kattako mongsonge ra-ode aro 
knatimenggipa mandeni gisik nanganiko ua kattao dingtangmancha 
ga-akatna skode, katta mingsa ba minggnini ja-mantapan dondikaia 
aro conunako jakkala. Howard Denison W. Mominni “A-chik Ku-rang” 
poedooniko ritinggni saloke nina: 
“Chipat, A-chik me-a me-chik, 
Bidan’ gunko gingsikchina”. 
lano skanggipa ritingo katta mingbrisan aro uko dondikgijan 
mingna man-aigen. Aroba, “Chipat A-chik me-a me-chik” ine comma 
on-gija poraioba, ritingni miksonganiko ma-siaia. “Chipat” kattani

ja-mano comma on-jaode, ritingo on-gipa pilak kattarangni gamchata 
apsan. Indiba Denison “Chipat” kattani ja-mano comma on-anichi, 
dondikkubo, ia kattani gimin dikdiksa chanchie nikubo, ine 
poraigiparangko ba knatimenggiparangko didienga. 
REGISTER : Ku-agananio register-ba mongsonggipa ong-a. 
Songsaro janggi tangon dal-a raka, chona dal-a, skia-poraia donga, 
skia poraia dongja, nana rokomni gadangni manderang dongbrin 
tangbrina. Gadangni kri agananiko register ine agana. Mesokani gita 
“asonga” kattako ra-na. Dedrangko, ripengskarangko ba an-tangna 
chonbatgiparangko asongna aganon, “asong” ine aganoba ong-aia. 
Indiba dal-bat rakbatgipana chokki on-e “asong” ine aganode, uan 
mande ra-gijani aro ka-anangatani ong-gen. Asonypabo ba asong bo 
inosa gadangni kri kraa. Indakegadangkonie katta jakkalania register 
ong-a. 
8 
RHYME: Dictionaryo seani gitade, “rhyme" kattani 
miksonganiara, “sameness of sound of the two or more words at the 
end oflines orverse.” Podprako ritingantinibonkamgipa kattarangni 
apsan gam-aniko ba ma-rapaniko iano miksonga. Poedo mingantion 
ritingrang apsangen indake ong-ja. Badiaba poedoni podprako 
ritingrangbang-ja, banobara bang-a; uni krisa ritingrangko ma-rapata. 
Ma rapaniara poedoko mingtoata, knatoata aro gisik ra naba 
altubatata. 
Ma rapatani rokomrang bang-a; poedo segipani skaosa aro 
namnikaosa pangchakaia. Mitamrang ritingrangko joljol ma-rapata 
aro mitamrangara wengwatgrike ba dedagrike ma-rapata. Ramkhe 
W. Mominni “A-ako Dakani” poedoni skanggipa podko nina: 
"A-chikranga agantoka a-’ko dakaniko, 
Nostu Nopantu minga a-’ko ba-gipako, 
Setirini bibal balna chong-kabamitingko, 
Samtokani bite nangna situbachengako.” 
la podo ritingbri donga; ia ritingbrian “-ko” katta-tongo aro “o” 
gam-anio bon-a. Skanggipa gam-ani “o”-na, oikor “a” ko on-na. 
Ritingbrian “o” gam-anio bon-ani gimin, oikor “a” -kon changbri sena 
nanggnok. Indake ritingbrini ma-rapani bewalara a a a a ong-a.

Modhunath G. Mominni bimingo nakatgipa “Meserangni Melaa” 
poedoni skanggipa podko niskana: 
“Salantian menggo mangsa meserangko chike, 
Bon-angatengachim ina pilakkon indake, 
Uni gimin meseranga namen jajrengaha, 
Menggooni jokna cholko tom-e melaaha.” 
la podoba ritingbri donga. Skanggipa ritinggni ma-rapa, maina 
uarang “e” gam-anio bon-a. la gam-anina an-ching “a” oikorko on-na. 
Gittamgipa aro brigipa ritingrangba ma-rapa, maina uarang “a" 
gam-anio bon-a. Ia gam-anina “b” oikorko on-skana. Ma-rapani 
bewalde indake ong-a: a a b b. 
Couplane G. Mominni “Saksan” poedoo dingtang ma-rapani 
bewalko nika: 
9 
“Ka-san’ walsare ching-chaenga 
Unan ka-saa nape; 
Indioba aganjaenga- 
Anga dongnok grape.” 
Kosako talatgiminko ja-rike seode, ia podni ma-rapani bewalara 
a b a b ong-a. 
Ma-rapani bewalko M. Rongmithuni “Pante Bilsi Chi-chet” 
poedooniko podsa ra-e, ma-rapatani bewalko nichotna. 
“Be-en bimang ritchenga, 
Gisik ka-tong an-senga, 
Pante bilsi chi-chet, 
Kato ja-rakmiting, 
Re-o gong-rakmiting, 
Neng-a dongja rektek”. 
Kosako gitan nitaiode, ia podo ma-rapani bewalara a a b c c b. 
Pilak poedorangkon ma-rape sea indake ong-ja. Mitam 
poedorangode, mongsongbate chasong gitalni seanirango, 
ma-rapanina mikpakma nija. 
IMAGE Aro IMAGERY: Dictionaiyo gitade “image”-ara “close 
likeness; counterpart; mental picture or idea; concept of somebody 
or something.” Image inode bimang apsansrangaianiko gisiko nikani

ba chanchianiko aro maikoba ba sakoba gisiko nikate ra-aniko 
mangsonga. Image kattako verb gita ra-ode, noksa sala gita gisiko 
nikata, mesokpila ba gisiko nikjagringatna man-gipa chinko ba 
bimangko on-a ine miksonga. landakgipa Aganani Bimangrangko sea- 
jotanio imagery ine agana. 
Jonmoni D. Shirani “Basako” poedoni ritinggniko nina: 
“Dongenga gamrang rong-kolo gitasa, 
Ong-katgen u’rang badia salosa?” 
lano apsananggipa bosturangko imagetyko jakkale tosusaaniko 
nika. Skia-poraiani bidingo ja-manchake, andalao dongenggipa A-chik 
A-songko andalgipa rong-kolming toa. Gunrang,changa-sapanirang 
dongoba uarangko lekka-porachi wa-saatgija jakkalgija donenganiko, 
rongkolni andalao dongenggipa gamrangming Jonmoni toa. Indake 
Jonmonini imageryko jakkale seaniara poraigiparangko 
nikjagringatna aro Achikrangni obostako nipilate name ma-sie ra-na 
dakchaka. 
POETIC JUSTICE; Poetic justice inon “ideal justice, with 
proper distribution of rewards and punishments” ine miksonga. 
Chong-motan, kragipa bichalani, jeon namgipana boksisko aro 
namgijagipana sastiko on a. Poedorango aro songsarni golporango 
denggugipa denggutangna, paptangna ba maiba ong-gija dakanina 
maming sastiko manjaode, poetic justice-an ba kraa bichal ba kakket 
bichal dongja ine agana. 
POETIC LICENCE ; “Poetic licence” katta dolkoba sea- 
jotanirango nika. Poedo segiparang a'’o songsarni golpoko segiparang, 
antangtangni seanirango meli-ma gapatanina aro riting meliatna gita 
grammami niamrangko olgrokata aro kattani ortoko on-tisa jujaata. 
Indiba jegita dingtangate seoba, maioba melianide dongtelaia. 
Dingtangatani ba on-tisa jujaataai bilko uamang an-tangtangna on-a. 
lachi uamangni seanirang me-sudapatako man-an baksana 
poraigiparangnaba gisikni rt "ulko on-a. Indake segiparang salgiko 
a-sak aro a-sakko salgi dakata; salgio aram gitchoani palo sagalo jahas 
re-ako nikatskaata. Do-o mutkoba uamang ku-aganata aro mandekoba 
miteming apsanata. 
Lindrid D. Shirani ki-tapni katta bichong “Gisik Matgrikrang”

ingipako gisik ra-atna. Lindrid Shira sea-jotanio mingsinggipa 
A-chikrangni gimin ia ki-tapo seaha. Uni seoke segipa manderangna 
ia katta bichong “Gisik Matgrikrang” krachong-mota. Matgrik inon 
be-eno bimango gipinrangna batakosan, bilaka-wa-saako aro 
ka-dongakosan miksongaija. Gisik wa-sagipa, chanchiao jera ra*ao 
gisik matsramgiparang ong-paa A-chik maaderangba. Shira poetic 
licenceko chol ra-e, “Matgrikrang” kattako jakkale, katta bichongko 
wa-saata aro ning-o seako poraina gisik sala. Imageryko jakkale

seaniara, seaniko me subatata. landake seani bewalara poedo,

AGANANI BIMANGRANG 
(Figures of Speech) 
Aganchengani 
Pilak janggi gnanggiparangan ma maltangni ning o uiatgrikani 
ba ma siatgrikani cholrang ba chinrangko jakkala. Do bimani 
mikoanikon do bisarang cha ani niken okamatengama ba bobilko 
niksoesa an tangona okamatengama aro joke katna chol am chinasa 
mikrakatengama uia. Matburingrangba apsankon daka. Achakrang 
nokgipako niksoon ki me gon gone ka sariri sing a; a gittal re-baode 
gaogaopile singa. Achakskarang rebaode ajatangko kange mesoka. 
Buringni matburingrang su budape ba maiba bibako ong-katate 
a dam kange sima ari ma-ate dona. Biap a dam ra ani, bitip ra*ani 
aro jika-sea ra ani somoekoba uarang chinrangchi aro ring-achi- 
mikoachi iiiatgrika. Indiba ia chinrang aro cholrangara 
chong diksasan; chu soksranggipa ong ja; bang-aba bang ja. 
Ma-maltangoni manggipinna krabate, nambate aro nitobate 
uiatgrikani cholrang uarango dongja. 
Mande jat iano matburingrangoni namen dingtangbea. 
Manderangode saksa sakgipinming agangrikani ba uiat- 
ma-siatgrikani nambegipa, niam ja-rike tarigipa chol gnang. la chol 
ramram ong ja; uan ning-tue chanchie tarigimin bewalsa ong a. 
Matburingrangni uiatgrikani bewalkode “Language” ba “Kusik” ine

aganna man-ja. “Ku sik” inon songsarni mande dolsani ba toksani 
an-tangtangni gisepo saksa sakgipinni aganako ma-sie agangrikna 
jakkalgipa, name tik ka grike, cholrangko ba chinrangko sulsul 
tarigiminkosa miksonga. 
Mandeni saksa sakgipinko uiatgrikna jakkalgipa cholrangara 
knana man gipa aro nikna man gipa ong a. Knana man gipa inon 
ku-agananiko aro nikna man-gipa inon seaniko mangsonga. Mande 
an-tangni aganani aro seani bewalko, matburingrang gita ong-gija, 
nambatatna ba knatobatatna man*a. Gajetbatatna aro donmitape 
aganna ba sena man a. Knatobate, ga*jetdape aro me*subatate 
agananio ba seanio mande “Figures of Speech” ba “Aganani 
Bimangrang”-ko jakkala. Aganani Bimangrangko golpo ba poedo 
13 
segiparang jakkalrongbata. 
Rhetoric kattani gimin iana skanggipa seanio janapaha. Seanio 
ba ku-agananio Ku-sikko rongtale aro tik ong-e jakkalaniara mandeni 
mingsa changa-sapani ba gun ong-a. Rhetoricara Ku-sikko name 
knatoe jakkalani bewalrangko sulsul tik ong-atanian. 
Nitodapatanirangko jakkale, gisil gitolrangko pate nomil an-tangko 
nitodapata gitan, ma-marikkit aganani aro seani cholrangchi aro 
bimangrangchi Ku-sik jakkalaniko me-subatatna, knatobatatna aro 
ga-kimbatatna man-a. 
Grammarde Ku-sikni ja-pang bikrok. Sentenceo katta sulsul 
donani, katta rikani, sentenceo kattarangni nangrimgrikani-iarang 
pilaknan niamrang donga. la niamrangko Grammarsa tik ong-ata. 
Sentenceo kattarangko sulsul name donjaode, kattarangni 
nangrimgrikani niamko jakkaljaode aro katta mingprakna tik ka-e 
dongimm oikorrangko jakkaljaode, seaniko ba agananiko ma-sina 
neng-gen. Aganani Bimangrangko jakkalgija aganoba ba seoba 
sentericeko ma-siade ma-siaia; indiba uko namilong dake nikaia. 
Aganani Bimangrangko kraa gita jakkale aganosa ba seosa, aganani 
ba seaniko chola ba-ra krae nitoe gana gita nika aro 
bimangchongchong nikgijagipakoba nilgagringata. 
Figure of Speech-ko Dictionaryo, “expression that gives variety 
or force, using words out of their literal meaning”, ine orto on-a.

Chong-motan, kattarangni orto manchaoni re-jite uarangko 
jakkalanichi rokom dingtangani aro ga-kimaniko on-e aganani ba 
seanian “Aganani Bimangrang”-ko jakkalani ong-a. 
Ku-sikni ga-kimani aro knatoaniko seanio ba agananio jakkalgipa 
rokomarichol-bewalrangchibariatna man-a. Aganani Bim an grangko 
jakkalaniba indakgipa chol-bewal mingsa ong-a. 
English Ku-siko Aganani Bimangrang bang-bea. Apsan 
Bimangrangko A-chikkuo aganani ba seanio jakkala ba 
mitamrangkode jakkaljama aro gipin chol-bewalrangko jakkalskaama 
maming sandirikkite niani dongkuja. Englisho Aganani 
Bimangrangko ma-manti bidingrango pangchakate ka-mao on-a gita 
ma-mal daka: 
1. Apsan Ong-ani (Similarity) 
14 
II. Meliani ba Nangchappaani (Association) 
III. Dingtanggrikani (Difference) 
IV. Chanchijagringani (Imagination) 
V. Tongtong Ong-gijani (Indirectness) 
VI. Gam-ani (Sound) 
VII. Sentence Rikani (Construction) 
la mingsniko sulsul nie nina. Segimin mesokanirangko 
man-gijagiparango, banaitaigiparangko iano jakkala. 
I. APSAN ONG-ANIO PANGCHAKATGIPA AGANANI 
BIMANGRANG. 
la tokni ning-o man-gopgiparang: 
1. Simile (Srangsrang Tosusaani) 
2. Metaphor (Donchipile Tosusaani) 
3. Allegory (Donmitapgipa Bakrogipa Golpo) 
4. Parable (Toromni / Cholon Bewalni Bidingo Skidapani 
Donggipa Kan-dikgipa Golpo) 
5. Fable (Srangsrang Skidapani Donggipa 
Kan-dikgipa Golpo) 
1. SIMILE (SRANGSRANG TOSUSAANI) 
Simile katta Latin katta “Similis”-oni ong-kata, jeni ortoara 
apsangjpa ba apsan ong-ani . Simileara rokom dingtanggipa bostu

minggnini apsan ong-aniko rongtale ba srangsrang janapanian ong-a. 
Uan rokom dingtanggipa bostu minggnini gun apsananiko tosusaani 
ong-a. Similenija-panggunrangara indake ong-a:

2. METAPHOR (Donchipile Tosusaani) 
Metaphor Greek katta “meta” aro “phero”“ko ra dime ong atgimin 
katta ong a. “Meta”-ni orto dingtanga aro “phora”/ “phero” ni orto 
“Ango gnang” ba “Anga ra bita”. Miksonganiara, tosusaaniko 
paraksranggija ra bitchipile dongaia. Ia Aganani Bimango, katta 
mingsako minggipinni biapona ra-angskaa, jeni a-selan tosusaani

srangsrang ong-gija napchipile dongaia. Uni gimin, maimancha 
apsananio pangchakate tosusaa, uko talsranggija rim-chipe ra na 
ga aka. Indake Metaphorko Compressed Simile ba Bakkandikatgimin 
Simile ineba agana.

Tosusaani).

Tosusaaniara kra-melipilgijagipa, bebera na man-ani nalsao 
ongpilgipa aro che-emdugagipa ongna nangja. Metaphor biaptangna 
kragipa tosusaani ong-na nanga. Uigija ba aganronggija 
bidilrangoniko kattarangko ra*e, maming apsananiko mesokgijagipa 
tosusaaniko jakkalaniara, agananiko ba seaniko namdapatna ba

knatodapatna bilmanja. Indake kra-melipilgijagipa tosusaanikon 
Strained / Forced Metaphor ba Bilgrigipa Tosusadraani ine agana.

3. HYPALLAGE BA TRANSFERRED EPITHET 
(ADJECTIVEKO BA TALATENGGIPA KATTAKO 
RA-JUJAANI) 
la Aganani Bimango, talatenggipa katta tokko ba talatgipa Epithet 
ba Adjectiveko, katta mingsaoni minggipinona ra-jujaa. Mandeko 
talatenggipa kattako sentenceo bostuko talatna jakkalskaa. la 
ra-jujaenggipa kattara gnigipa kattaming meli-ma-gapsrangade 
ong-ja; talatgipa katta skanggipa kattamingsa meli-ma-gapa. Talatani 
kattako gnigipa bostuming jakkalo meli-nangrimanide agangipani ba 
segipani gisikosan dongaia. 
Mesokani : Mikgrigipa ja-arang re-a norokchi. 
lano ja-arangde mikgrija; mande ba uni gisiksa mikgria. lano 
talatgipa kattara mikgrigipa aro uko mandeoniko uni ja-arangona 
ra-angskaa.

11. HYPERBATON BA INVERSION fKATTARANGKO 
GRAMMARNl GITA SULSUL DONANIKO DONJUJAANH 
Hyperbatono Grammarni niamko olgroke ra-a. Grammar gitade 
Achikkuo Subject skanga, Object jatchia aro Verbdeja-mana. Indiba 
gasudapani aro ga-kimanina mikpakma nie, sentenceo Grammar gita 
kattarangko sulsul donaniko agangipa ba segipa ja-rilga. 
Mesokani : Gamko dal niknabe na-simang. 
la sentenceni subject “na-simang” ja-mana, Object “gamko” 
skanga aro Verb “dal niknabe” jatchia. Sentenceo objectko 
donchengode, ua kattaan mongsongbatgipa ong-a ine mesoka.

biap ra-jujaa.

segipa agana. Indake tosusagrike seaniara, skidapaniko ga-jetdapatna

la seanina anga A-chik poedo segiparang sak lo-kosan ra-aiaha. 
la poedo segiparangni janggi tangani, chu-gimik sea-jotanina 
on-gilani, ba seani rokom bewalni bidingko iano man-chapatja. 
Uamangni poedorango tosusana jakkalgipa kattarangni bidingkosa 
iano niaia. la sakchikking poedo segiparangara: Gilbert K. Marak, 
Howard Denison W. Momin, Evelyn R. Marak, Mackenson

lamangni poedorangkoba chappa ka-atgiminrangkosan ra-aia. 
1. GILBERT K. MARAKNI POEDORANG 
(i) Salniangan Ku-rang: 
Gilbert K. Marakni poedo ki-tap “Ang Gisik Ku-aning” Bidol I-o 
nakatgipa poedorangko nie nina. Poedo segipa “Salniangan Ku-rang”- 
0 salna mandeni kuaganani bilko on-e uko mande gita aganata: 
‘Kai! Re angengane angade; 
Agilsak seng-atbo an-tangko 
Tochrikoba ku-manate, uko Salgrana aganchakata: 
‘Gitel tengatgen ang’ ama gita’ 
Chonbegipa tochrini a-gilsakko ama dipet teng-atna Salgrana 
ku-chakaniara, mande sakprakan chongipa remgipa ong-oba 
a-songtangko nikseng-jasengatna man-a ine mesokani ong-a. Salgrani 
aganangakoba poedo segipani nachilsa knarikaia. 
(iil Nokdang: 
GObert “Nokdang” poedoo ka-saaniko bitchil baksa toa. Bitchil 
chae, ja-pang ja-dil ong>kate, chekwate, bijak ringsole, manchoke stue 
biteo nakatea. 
“Ka-saani bitchri mandeo ge-jok, 
Uni ka'tongo chababebejok”. 
Ka*saani mandeni ka-tongo ja'dil su-e, dal-roroe, wa*saroroe 
50

kimgrikaona soka aro demikkang nikao nokdang chu sokea. Indake 
nambee Gilbert ka-saaniko bolming toa. 
fijil Rasoneni Kimbriona: 
“Rasongni Kimbriona” poedoo, Gilbert miksongaona sokna 
krenganiko a-bri doaming toa. Miksongani-ni bimang dongja, indiba 
akimbriko gisiko donate poedo segipa una bimang on-a. Akimbriona 
soknade neng-e dona nanga, ramao namgija biaprang, 
ja-bikbakatanirang donggen; uarangko amesa uonade sokna man-gen. 
Miksonganiona soknaba bang a neng-nikanirangko aro duk 
saknaanirangko chagrongna nanggen. Uni ja-manosa rasong 
mikkimko an-tangna kangna man-gen. 
“Chubate, chubate, chubate, 
Doangbo, me-tra pante”. 
Poedo segipa ia apsan poedoon mandeni nama gunko bibalni 
similaming toa. Similgipa bibalni biba samtangtango gipa gita, 
mandeni rasong mikkim aro uni nama gunko pilakchinan janganga 
ine poedo segipa agana. 
(iv) W al-ki Go pa: 
“Wal-ki Gopa” poedoo, Gilbert ning-tugipa tosusaaniko daka. 
Kangal manderang dislai ra-taitaina man-ja aro skangode dislaian

ning-o gope dona. Chu-dapo wa-al sia gita nikoba, tappra ning-o wa-al 
tange donga. lako poedo segipani tosusaaniko nina: 
“Namgipa manderangba 
Donga a-gUsakoba; 
Indiba pap pipripa. 
Namgijarang pindapa;

Jensalo Nokgipa an-tangna biltaigen,

Sigija jringjrotni janggiko man-taigen”. 
la ritingrango, namgipa manderangko wal-ki tangaming aro 
uamangni samtangtango donggipa paparigipa namgijagipa

apsanon, wa alko aro tapprako ra bitdime donggipa chu dapko nama

M Sal Kandikbea : 
la poedoo, poedo segipa kan-dikgipa janggi tanganio a gilsako

fvil Chame Saljagring :

(vii) Namgya Ripeng: 
“Namgija Ripeng” poedoo Gilbert namgija ripengko 
jagringming toa. 
“Ripeng, namgija ripeng, 
Ong a ua jekai nang ni jagring”. 
Jagring mandeoni ekja: ua mandeko ja rikstapaia. Uandake 
namgija mandeba lap ropna maiba mandapanina ba uko dakmajona 
namgipa mandeko ja rika. 
Bon-kamgipa podo iako sea: 
“Gimaa jekai jagring salakimo; 
Dukni salo dakchalga ua ripeng. 
Manjawaha uko amo.” 
Sal rakosa jagring nakata; salakimode dongja. Salakmitingko 
mandeni obosta namaniming aro salakimaniko mandeni duk 
jajrenganiming iano toa. Mandeni cha an dongan somoeo ripeng 
bang-a, indiba uni rasongko duk salakimdapon uni namgija 
ripengrangba gimaangpaa. la poedoo chanchiani “Ripeng 
Chongmot”-o chanchianiming apsana.

(xiii) Mingsamangmang :

la poedoo mandeni chanchianiko

Sikkatrika

(xv) M^ioani: 
“Majoani” poedo ia kattarangchi a-bachenga:

“Majoani soka 
Chi dal-a gita”. 
Chi dal'ania manderangna dal-a dukniko ra-baa; mitamde chi

ia poedoo chi dal-animing toa. Majoani sokbaoba mitamrang uko 
gelna ba amna man-a. Gitelko jechakgipa Pitorko poedo segipa ia toko

Judas, ine poedo segipa agana. Judas tangkana mikboke 
dakmajoanichin ama man-aha; indake ua Jisu Kristoko siaona 
galonaha.

(xxiv) Nepoleon:

“Indiba dakaia ketcha 
Do-bipa gita gangchacha.”

dakgrikaia. Nepoleonni siwing-sikap dakaiani gimin poedo segipa uko 
ketcha baksana do-bipaming toa.

fxxxil Misi Ringring Panteko Singra; 
la poedoo sea gitade, Misi Do-me Baljagring nomUa, 
“Dakaiana okgila te-mina mina,

lanoba nomilni an-gilko te-e minaming totaia aro peteng

chananga aro do-mesal gita chi-jenge, gisikba wa-sae donga ine Gilbert 
miksonga.

ku-dime ku-rachakatangko pe-na nangjachim ina. Namgija gisik 
me-chiko Hobaoni napani gimin, poedo segipa me-chikko gisik 
namgijani “cholguga” minga. Poedo segipa an-tangni gisik duk 
ong-beaniko ka-tongo ‘biji’ su-kame saknaaniming toa. 
(viii) Ka-saa : 
Mackenson “Ka-saa” poedoo ka-saaniko bolming toa. Bol ja-dil 
su-chenge dal-roroe, cheksirang bipekroroe, apale cheksi bijak 
badalrikrike uarangni ka-mao donggiparangko ganggopa: 
“Ro-angripet cheksi badalroroa.” 
Ka-saaniba saniba ka-tongo napahaon, cheksipe bariroroa: 
“Ka-sara cheksipe plakchin gipa,

Bringripat tike plakna tika.” 
74 
Ka-saani saksaosan bon-aija; ka^saani saksa sakgipinni kosako

man-ja, chichi mripatna manja ine Mackenson agana. 
“Ka-sara Pringprang Attampang Aski, 
U’de gopramona kingkingan tengaiengpiti”. 
Pring seng-mitchiahaon Pringprang Aski natelaia; 
attampangahaoba Attampang Asld natelaia . la Aski dongkamaia gitan 
ka-saaniba grikjang dongaia, pangnaba tipja ine poedo segipa toe 
agana: 
“Ka-san’ ka-tong wal sare kama dare dare 
Man-ja dokkimitna uko dal-bea chidare. 
Gisikni pUak mikbokako, 
Namgija pilak chanchiako, 
Ka-tongni pilak mitchiako, 
Uasa-an kame gala po-one, 
Bon-rokrok bon-toktoke.” 
Mackenson ka-saani bilko wa-alni bilmingba toa. Mikkango 
donggipakode wal-sare jekoba kamgalaia. Uandake, ka-saanichi, 
ka-saa kattachi ba kamchi, gisikni pilak chanchianirangko amsrangna 
man-a ine ua agana. 
“Ka-saa dongjaode mandeo, a-gilsak a-gisi, 
Maidake tangkugen janggia maiachi?” 
lano ka-saani grigipa ka-tongko sama bola gngipa do-a mata

gnanggiparang tangna amja. Uandaken mandeo ka-saani griode, 
janggi tangani maming namgni gri, uo maming gisik an-sengani 
katchaani dongja.

AGANCHENGANI 
Bilsiantian March 8 -ko A gilsak Gimikni Me chikrangni Sal ine 
mania. Bilsi 2000-ko Me-chikrangko Bilmanatani Bilsi ine maniaha. 
Me*chikrangna, mongsongbate A-chik me-chikrangna mande ra-ani 
aro mittelani pal gita, anga iako sea. 
A chikkuo poedo segipa A-chik me-chikrangoni jakchomsana 
agre, bang-akon poedo segiparang ine aganna man-ja. Poedo mingsa 
ba minggnikosan seari dontongaigiparang bang-a. Mingsa minggni 
poedorangoniko segiparangni chanchianirang, uamangni gisikni 
re-ani, seani rokom-bewal aro uarang gitako toe aro tosusae nina 
neng-a. 
Badiaba poedorango seani ning-tua, seani rokom-bewal ga-sua; 
indiba uarangko segiparang poedo mingsa ba minggnini ja-manon 
jripjrang dakaijok. Mitamrangni jako segimin poedorang dongenggen, 
indiba uarangko chappa ka-a^askalde dongja gitan ong-aia. Chappa 
ka-atjani gimin uamangni kosako mamingkoba agannade man-taija, 
maina uarangko jinmana separakatna gita A-chikkuo magazinerang, 
journalrang dongja. Magazine kingsa kinggni nakatoba na-chi 
mitimaia. Indake poedorangkosan ong-aigija, gipin seanir an gkoba 
parakatna chol dongja.

A-CHIKKU ROMANTIC POETRY 
Seani bidingo maiba apsan gunrangko mesokgipa seanirangko 
aro segiparangko apsan toko donronga. Indake A chikku Romantic 
Poetry inon, A-chikkuo poedo segiminrang aro uarangko segiparang

apsan gunrangko mesokna nanggen. Romantic Poetry-ni gunrangko 
mesokgipa poedorangko aro uarangko segiparangko ia 
chanchirimaniona ra*baa. Saniba poedo mingsamangmangosan ia 
gunko nikaiode poedo segipani gisikni re-aniko aro uni seani bidingko 
tosusana gita uni gipin seanirang donggijanio, uandakgipa seaniko 
ia seanini a-palo donaiaha. 
A-chikku Romantic Poetry-ko chanchirimna skpng, sea-jotanio 
“romance”, “romanticism” kattaranga maiko mangsonga aro 
Romantic Poetry-o man-chapatna gita seanio mai gunrang dongna 
nanga an-ching nichengna. “Romance” kattakora dingtangrikrilcna 
man-gipa, indiba ja-pang ja-dilde apsan dongaigipa gunko talatna 
jakkala. Janggi tangchongmotanioni dingtangsranggipa 
chanchianirang aro gisikni re-anirangko talatnaba iako jakkala. 
“Romanticism” inon, gisikni/ ka-tongni uina man-ani wa-sagipa bil 
(sensibility) aro gisiko wa-sabee noksa dake ch'anchiatani bil 
(imagination) ba gunko mangsonga. Olgroke ra-ode, “romanticism” 
inon ong-chongmotana badepile donaniko aro gisikni/ ka-tongni 
re-anirangko ning-tubee janapanichi sakgipinni gisiko/ ka-tongo 
su-dikeaniko mangsonga. Walter Patemi chanchianio ia kattani ning-o 
minggni bidingrang re-chapa. Mingsara “nitoani” (beauty)-na ka-saani 
aro minggipinara “nitoani”-ko kosakgilgil niaigija uni ning-o dongnue 
donggipa maiba bil ba gunko uie-ma-sie ra-na sikani. 
ComptonRickett-ni chanchiani gita, “Romanticism”-ni asol 
gunrangara iarang: (i) a-palo nikaigipa gun ba bilni badeao dongnue 
donggipa gun ba bilko ma-siani; (ii) gisik/ taningni gita uie-ma-sie 
ra-na sikbeani; (iii) janggi tanganio ramrambegipa indiba ja-pang- 
asol gunrangko ui-ma-siani aro uko parakani. 
Nigope niode, iarangan Romantic Poetryni gunrang ong-a: 
1. A-palo nikaigipa gun ba bilni badeao dongnue donggipa gun 
ba bilko ma-siani: la ma-sianio nikrong nironggija bilko mande ra-an 
baksa una kenani, nikronggimin-uigiminna aiao inmankuani aro 
nitoaniko a-sakni parakanirangko gisik-ka-tong rongjrime 
rachakanirang rebrina. lano Ongchongmotaniko (Reality) jegalja; 
Ongchongmotani iano donga. Indiba uko gisik/ katongni reani gital 
gunrangchi ba bidingrangchisa dingtange nikataia.

2. Gisik/ taningni gita uie-ma-sie ra-na sikbeani: Nikrong- 
nironggija bilko mande ra an baksa una kenani, nikronggipa- 
ui giminn aba aiao inmankuani aro nitoaniko a-sakni parakanirangko 
mikronchisan ong-aigija gisik-ka-tongo nia-nikani dongon, gisik 
taning wa-sabatani aro ning-tubate uie-ma-sie ra-na sikbeani 
dongtelaia. 
3. Janggi tanganio ramrambegipa indil>a ja-pang-asol gunrangko 
ui-ma-siani: Iano mandeni mande ong-aniko ra-chakani, mandeni 
ka-saani kosakgilgil ong-aigija, mikgittal a-gilsakni paksachinaba 
re-badeangani aro a-gilsakni ramrambegipa bosturangomangba 
dongnue dongenggipa dingtangmanchagipa gunko ba bilko nikani/ 
ma-sianiko man-gopa. Songjinmako jegale, songadamni 
tom-tomanina, jong-pugijanina rikani, Ong-telaigipa A-gilsakni 
ka-bakona pil-angpOani ia gunrangba niksengen donga. 
Romantic Poetiyni ia gunrangko ra-e A-chikku Romantic Poetiyni 
gimin nina. Namedake chanchie nion ia A-chikku Romantic Poetiyko 
seani somoe 1930 jolni bon-angachipak aro 1940-rangni 
a-bachenggipa bilsirangos£m ong-bataia. la somoenija-mano A-chikku 
Romantic Poetry-ko seanirang rittak rittaksan dongaia aro saksani 
seanirangoni badiaba mingsaosan Romantic Poetiyni gunko nikaia. 
Indake uandakgipa poedorangko aro uarangko segiparangko iano 
man-chapatna jakkeja. 
An-tangni chanchie aro poraie ra-anio pangchake anga iano 
iamang sak 5 (bonga) kosan A-chik Romantic Poetrang ba A-chikkuo

KAN-DIKGIPA GOLPORANG 
Kan*dikgipa Golporangni A*bachengani 
Short Stories ba kan dikgipa golporang skangsrangonin 
dongachim. Buring samring re jojoe sikar ka e janggi tanggiparang, 
sikar ka-mitingo uamang mairangko nika, mai obostarangko 
chagronga, uarangko chankolsamo nokni manderangna aro wal-tim 
chakao songsul-noksulni ba songni gipin manderangna agane rona 
namnikbeachim. Basakobade matburing man-bagipako maidake 
changa, bano tima aro bon-kamao maidake so-ota uarangko agane 
rotaina kusi ong-a. Jokatbagipakode mangrogijako mangroate, 
chonbeakon dal-dapate aganbadee uamang rasong chaa aro biltang 
jaktango gaora de-a. landake chankolsamo ba wal-timo agane 
roanirangonisa kan-dikgipa golporang ong-katahakon ine 
chanchichipani gnang. 
Golpo aganna ba knatimna teng-atanirangko ba asong- 
ge-songchakanirangko nangja. Wal-timni wal-sare ching-chakao a-pao 
asonge knatimna man-aia. Golponi su-uanian knatimgiparangko sin-a 
ding-aniko aro ok chi-otaniko dikdiksana gualata. Knatimenggipa 
bi-sarangoni mitamrang dal-ahaon golpo agangiparang ong-baskaa. 
Indake golporang chasongsaoni chasongsaona tangrikrika. la 
aganrikrikgipa golporang agangipani, somoeni aro obostani kri katta

bichongo, golponi re-anio, aganani bewalo aro bon-atanio jringna 
apsanja. Salantini janggi tanganio nie-nike ra-giparang aro gital 
obostarang golpo ong-baa aro gitchamrangna ritchudapani ba 
uarangoniko ra-galani ramram ong-pila. Ja>manosa, bimang done 
kan-dikgipa golporangko seani bewal nakatbaaha. Ka-sine ka-sine 
seani rokom, golponi bimang aro ma-marikkit katta bichongrangko 
jakkalani nambatrikrike, chasong gitalni kan-dikgipa golponi 
gadangona sokdoaha. 
Kan*dikgipa Golporangni Bang*rikrikani 
Kan-dikgipa golporang nakatchengon, poraigiparang uarangko 
dal-nikjachim aro uarangna gisik on-ani grichim. Songsarni 
golporangko segiparangni mingsa ro-gipa songsarni golpoko semane 
minggipinko a-bachengna skang, dikdiksa choljokao see rogipa gitasa 
154 
kaii'dikgipa golporangko ra-aiachim. Bilsi 1900 jolosa kan-dikgipa 
golporangko seani rokom aro bewalara songsarni ro'gipa golpoko 
seaonide dingtangbea aro sena neng-bata ine manderang ina*sina 
a-bachengaha. Indakesa, kan-dikgipa golpoko seani, jatni sea-jotanio 
dingtangmanchagipa rokom ba ma mal dake biaptangko ra na 
a-bachengaha. 
la somoejolo kan-dikgipa golporangna dabiani nakataha aro 
baririkrikaha. Songbadrang aro magazinerangko chappa ka-ani 
bang-baton, kan-dikgipa golporangko man-chapatode uarangko 
palnangbata ine songbad-magazine chappa ka-atgiparang ma-siaha. 
Kan-dikgipa namnambegipa golporangko serakgjpa British mande 
Somerset Maughamni aganani gitade, ia golporangko songbadrango 
aro magazinerango ga^aode, uarangna dabiani komibegenchim aro 
segiparang sena gisik nangtokjawachim. 
Kan-dikgipa golporangni ta-rake barianiara songsarni 
golporangni bang-roroani giminba ong-a ine agana. Poraina 
namnikgiparang bang-roroaha aro ki-tap sualrorogipa libraryrangba 
bang-bataha. Una agreba, songsarni golporang, kan-dikgipa 
golporangko seani gital chol-budirangna do-ga oe on-aha. Aroba, 
manderangni poedoko dikdiksana namnikbrejamitingsomoeo poedo 
segiparangoni mitamrang kan-dikgipa golporangko seskaaha.

Franceo aro Russiao kan-dikgipa golporangko manderang namen 
ra-chaktokachim. French ku-siko Guy de Maupassantni golpo 
segiparang aro Russiao segiparangoni Tchekovni golporang Englando 
segipa golporangni kosako namen bilmanachim. 
Golporangchi A«soiigraiigko Kabinggrikani 
Chengoni golporangara atchu ambirangoniko ramram 
manderangni man-rikani ong-a. Mande nambata songsarko rikna 
jumang nika, nambata knalko man-na sika aro rasongtango 
chu-ongnikaniko man-ja. Ramram manderang an-tangtangni 
mikkangchina chanchisamsoanirangko aro ka-dongchaksoanirangko 
uamangni ring-anirango aro golporango paraka. Golpo aganna aro 
knatimna skani sakantion donga. Gisik nange knatime, "Unikora mai 
dakjok/ ong-jok?” ine sing-aniara knatimenggipani golpoko matchote 
uina skani baksa uni gisik nange knatimaniko mesokpila. 
155 
Knatimenggipani ia sing ani aro gisik nanganiara golpo 
aganenggipako wachaata. “Changsao...” inbaon, knatimenggiparang 
rang-sanan atchu ambini chasongona, dingtangmanchagipa 
a-gilsakona rimangako man-a. Ua chasongni golporangko knae, 
knagiparang ma-gitcham pagitchamrangni skanirangko aro 
ka-dongchaksoanirangko pe-rarone aro chasong gitalnirangming 
tosusae gital mikronrangchi, wa-sabata taningrangchi uarangko nia. 
Unikode bako galgen, bako kolamgen, badiako dakdape ra-gatgen 
uamang tik ka-a. Indake ia golporangchi batanggimin chasong aro 
da-o ong-enggipa chasongni gisepo kabinggrikaniko ong-ata. 
Golporangni nie<suanirang uarangni donmitapani gri ramram 
ong-anioni nakata. Un baksana manderangni janggi tangani, gisikni 
re-ani, skanirang aro gunrang apsananganichi a-songsako 
a-songgipinming kabinggrika. Kan-dikgipa golporangko badiaba 
jatnisa, gipin a-songnisa ine ma-ekgalja. A-gilsakni mande gimikan 
uarangko poraie apsan gisik an-senganiko man-a aro mande jatni 
janggi tanganina chanchiani ba simsakaniko mesoka. 
Kan'dikgipa Golporang^ii Dingtangani 
Ro-gipa songsarni golporango gitan golponi bikrok, 
mongsonggipa katta bichong, bak ra-giparang kan-dikgipa

golporangoba donga; indiba uaranga ma-kabata, kan-dikbata aro 
dingtang gadangosa ong-a. Kan-dikgipa golporangko seanirango 
mikpakma nianirang dingtang dingtang ong-na man-a. Uarangko 
segiparangoni mitamrangara plotna ba ja-pang golponi bikrokna gisik 
on-bata. English golpo segipa O. Heniyan golponi bikrokna mikpakma 
nigiparangoni saksa ong-a. Mitamara golponi bikrokkode mongsonge 
ra-na nangja inskaa. Kan-dikgipa golpoo ong-ronggimal a-bachengani 
aro bon-atani dongna nangja ineba Russian! golpo segipa Tchekov 
agana. Mitam golpo segiparang wa-sagipa kattarang aro gisik 
nangatgipa sentencerangko gamchatnikbatskaa. 1932 bilsio sea 
jotanina Nobel Prize man-gipa John Galsworthyni nikaniode, golponi 
sentenceantikon gisik nangatgipa ong-atna. man-ani bilsa 
naongsonggipa ong-a. 
Knn>dlkgipa Golponi Gunrang 
Kan-dikgipa Golponi dingtangmanchagipa gunrang uko ro-gipa 
156 
songsami golpooniko ma-ekata. Ua gunrangara: 
(i) Golponi miksonganini mingsasan ong^ani : Golpoo 
chu-sokatna nanggipa miksongani mingsasan ong^aina nanga. 
Songsarni golpoo odhaini odhai see, uarangko nangrimrimgrikate, 
ja-mano uarangko man-gopdimata. Kan-dikgipa golpoode iako dakna 
man-ja. Golponi ja-pang bikrok mingsasan ong-na nangaia. 
(ii) Dokchongipa apalani aro man gopani : Kan-dikgipa golpoo, 
mandeni maiba nie-nike ra-ani ba badiaba obostakosan niksengataia. 
Golpona nanggijagipakode man-chapatja. 
(iii) Bak ra-giparangni kam-cholon dingtang ong-anganiko 
mesokna chol donggijani ; Golpoko kan-dike ma-kete seani gimin aro 
obosta mingsakosan on-aiani gimin, namgijagipani namangani aro 
namgipani namanggijaniko mesokna chol ong-ja. Uni giminan, bak 
ra-giparang agre bang-jaan baksana, uamangni kam-cholonko name 
wa-saatna ba niksengatna nanga. 
(iv) Seani bewalni ma-ketani: Seani bewal ma-kagipa, 
nangchongmotgipakosan on-aigipa, gisik nangatna man gipa aro 
re-badegija name simsake kattarangko aro sentencerangko 
donsogimin arini ning-o jakkalgipa ong-na nanga. Mikrontangchi

nikako kakkete see on-ani bewalde chu-ongja, golpona namgipa 
bimangkoba on-na nanga ine Somerset Maugham agana. 
(v) Talataniko kan-dikatani : Ro-gipa songsarni golpoo, mande 
saksakon ba biap damsakon ro-prete talate rona man-a; indaka 
bewalde kan-dikgipa golpona cholija. Gimikkon akoti dakesa sena 
man-aia. 
(vi) Tongtong ong-gija, chanchianiko nakatate on-ani : 
Kan-dikgipa golpoo gimikkode tongtong talate on-ja; chanchichote 
ra-anikode poraigipanasa pakwata. 
(vii) Uina gitik dakatani aro bon-chotaniko pe-selani : Uina 
skaniko batatani, uina nanggipako uiatninggijani aro ka-dongsoaoni 
bikpile bon-ataniba kan-dikgipa golponi niksenggipa gun ong-a.

man a. Golporangara, ramram golpo aganaoni janggi tanganiming

brigiminrangming arc buchuma budeparangming, namgiparangming 
aro namgijagiparangming golpochi bakrimatako man-a. Badiaba

man-a. 
Janggi tanganini apalani gitan, katta bichong aro golponi ja-pang

Indiao Kan*dikgipa Golporangko Segiparang 
Indian! kan dikgipa golporang atchu ambini golporang, jatni 
gitcham golporang aro itihasni golporang ong a. Una agreba, 
dal dalgipa manderangni, rongtalgipa manderangni, a songtangna 
an tangtangko on kanggipa aro sichakgipa manderangni aro 
a-songko-jatko dQgiparangni golporangba donga. Ong-gijako ong-atna 
namgijako namatna, skidapani gnanggipa golporangba donga. 
Namgipa kam cholonni bewalrang, ka-dongani, dangdike on-ani, 
an-tangna chanchipilgija on-kangani, mandena ka-saani aro 
ka-sachakani, mittelpilskaani, chakchikani, ku-mong-nangrimani aro 
indakgipa gunrangko gamchate ra-atna aro niksengatna golpo 
seanirangba gnang. Rokomaii ong-ani aro rokom dingtangsranganiba 
ia kan-dikgipa golporangni niksenggipa gunrang ong-a. 
Indiao kan-dikgipa golporangko segiparangoni chugipa gadangko 
ra-giparangoni Rabindranath Tagore, R. K. Narayan aro Raja Raoba

ong-a. Rabindranath Tagore rokomari katta bichongrangko ra-e, 
rokomari ja-pang kattarangni gimin namen chanchianiko nakatate 
sena changa. Tagoreni kan-dikgipa golporangoni mitamko Wilson K. 
Marak A-chikkuona pe-skae, pe-skagimin poedorangko chape. 
"Rabindranath Tagoreni Basegimin Golporang aro Poedorang” ine 
katta bichong on-e, uko ki-tapni bimango nakatataha. R. K Narayanni 
golporang dingtangmanchagipa bewalko mesoka aro uarango gisik 
an-sengatani aro ka-dingani dongasan ong-aigija, ka-sachakatani aro 
duk ong-paataniba nakatronga.

KABENI JAPANG KATTA 
“Sigimin bimangna bon gimin janggina ka oksie ka a be e, 
tangmitingni somoerango nama dakgimin kam bewalrangko minge 
de-e aro ja-man ja>wilo dongrikan salrango mai obostarangko 
chagrongani ong gen, indake grap-mangtatani ba Kabeni sulchi 
aganjanapa,” ine Mihir N. Sangma uni kitap “Maniani Bidik”-o Oak-

49) agana. D. S. Rongmuthuni nikanio, Kabeo mandeni nambatgipa 
aro chubatgipa gunrangko, jekai kam-cholon namani, gisik ka-tong 
chuani, gisik kimkimani, kamtangna tosokpilgija on-kangani, 
jaksramani, ka-dongani aro kenanina gisik ka-tong mogijaniko 
mesoka. Sio-bon-ode mandeo maiba namgijani donggenchimoba 
ukode janapsrangja ineba ua agana.

rasong-mikkimo kusi ong-na aro rasong man-pana, salakim

Katta Jakkalani

simchi; chusudimua-tusidimua; ga-biranga-ga-branga; dikdik- 
dikdiksa; satti-satta ba chatta; giripa-gripa aro uagita.

chimonggipa Kaberango, gitalsranggipa kattarangko jotdapaniko 
nika. Jekai: dolbal, kasari, alwan, babu, saheb, raja, behali aro uagita.

An-chingni da-ororo aganrongbewal gitade, Skanggipao 
‘angkambe’-ni palo ‘nangkambe’-sa ong genchim. Gnigipaora, 
‘angkisang’-ni palo ‘nangkisang’-sa ong-genchim. 
Da omangba, Tura a-bri ja-reko donggiparangoni mitamrang, 
agangipa magipakosa janapengomangba ‘nangma’ ine aganengkua. 
Indake jakkaljujaani donggenoba, miksonganiko masianio maming 
peng-pangani dongja. 
Katta / Riting Mingtaitaiani 
Kabe ring-anio, badiaba kattarangko ba ritingrangko mingtaitaia. 
Mingtaipilgipa kattarang ritingni bon-kamgipa kattarang ong-bata. 
“Amana hajalsana chakat, chakat, 
Citing tasisana re-cha, re-cha.” 
(A-chikni Ku-andik) 
Apsan dake mingtaianiko “A-chikni Kuandik”-o nikkua:

“Gopja gisel chipange dongjok mamatangde, mamatangde, 
Chabi re-el tekange nijok o-gitode, o-gitode.” 
Degipani ma-ana kalime ring-anioba aro sigipani ma-ni-noni 
sigipana chu ra-baako kane grapmangtataoba, indake ritingsanon 
bon-kamgipa kattako mingtaipilaniko nika: 
(i) “Simak re-ruraboda, amara, amara.” 
(ii) “Adana salgrana ha, ring ring.” 
Katta minggniko mingtaipilaniko ka-mao on-gipa ritingrango 
nikskaa: 
“Kancha siljakjakcha, kancha siljakjakcha, 
Me-ga dingjalgakcha, me-ga dingjalgakcha.” 
(A-chikni Ku-andik) 
Katta ma-rap ma-rape mingtaitaianiara, bi-sa tusiatna lullabyko 
ba dingdedeko ring-e mumuaniming apsana. PQ-sa gita chanchiora, 
ka-a be-enggipa ma-a mikkangtango pujue donenggipa detangko tue 
dongenga gita daknike, una tusichina git ring-e on-tima gita daka; 
177 
maina ma-a detangni siako bebera-na neng-a aro ua simakpilgen 
ingipasa uni gisiko dongaia. 
Riting Meli-Ma-rapatani 
Kabe ring-anio sul-riting meli-ma-rapatna chol-bewal 
jakkalaniranga aiao inmanatani ong-a. Kabeni ritingrango katta 
tongrang badiabarango apsana, badiabarangora melisrangja; 
banobara tong bang-a, badiabaora tong komibea. 
A-chik Aganbewalrangni “Kabe”-ni ritingbriko nina: 
“Indakesa mite pe-a apako, 
Katchi wang-a do-mako. 
Rua basi te-rakcha apani angrikiko pe-a, 
Mrit samte so-jakcha do-mako wang-a.” 
lano ritinggnigni ma-rapa; indiba katta tongrangni bang-a 
bang-gijanide dingtanggrikbea. Skanggipa ritingo katta tong ge- ii, 
gnigipao ge- 7, gittamgipao ge- 16 aro brigipao ge- 11 donga. Ring-ode 
joljol ritinggniko sul meliata. lano skanggipa aro brigipa ritingrango

pangchakaigen. Indiba ia meliatani rokom-bewalko Kabeni 
ja-mangipa ritingrango ja-rikaba ong-skaja. 
Apsan Kabeni gipin ritingbriko niskana: 
“Angni sale cha-gipasaming, apasaming, 
Chingni de-e ringgipasaming, do-masaming. 
Bolong rakgipa gita apara, 
Bolsal den-ipa gita do-mara.” 
la ritingrango, skanggipa aro gnigipao katta tong ge- 13 prak; 
gittamgipa aro brigipaora ge- 10 prak donga. lana sul salanio 
neng-nikani dongjawa. 
Ka-mao on-gipa ritingbriara “A-chikni Ku-andik”- oni: 
“Sone andalangengjok sa-ra kin-dik dokrikbo, amara, 
Sa-ra kin-dik dokgimal; 
An-tam ka-sinangengjok sone ginchi galrikbo, sokchia, 
Sone ginchi galgimal.”

la ritingrangora, skanggipa aro gittamgipa katta tong bang-anio

ong-skagen. 
Katta sul meliatani chol mingsara ritingni badiaba kattako 
ro dapatani. Mesokani gita: tong ge- 3 donggina ea-brane-a kattako 
tong ge- 4 ga-bi-rang-a dakatskaa; ku-skia- ko ku-sik-ki-a . Katta 
ro-atna neng-ani dongode, sulko ro-atskaa ine raken rim-chipna

Kabe ring-anio knatimgipani gisik on-aniko rim-ketani minggipin

Ritingrangko, donmitapanio orto apsangipa kattarangko wengwat

Poedorango ritingni bon-ao donggipa kattarangchi ma-rapata. 
Kabeode, riting bijangchioba ma-rapataniko nika. Ka-mao on-gipa

(i) “Bolong rakgipa gita apara,

Bolsal den-ipa gita do-mara.” 
(A-chik Aganbewalrang) 
(ii) “Mangonani somoio nababone, Apara, 
Jamemangchi naango nisobone, Kawara.” 
(Maniani Bidik) 
(iii) “Birong chipinari gimaijokma, 
Sinchi binnekari rongbinaijokma.” 
(Pagitchamni Ku-bisring)

TOSUSAE AGANANIRANG 
Poedorango aro ring-anirango tosusae agananirangko jakkalbea. 
Tosusaania nilgagringaniko ba gisiko noksa dake chanchiataniko 
nakatate, mandeko bimangchongchong gita nikatna aro obostao

dongpamanchaenga gita daknikatna bil donga. landake tosusae 
agananio Kabe dal-dalgipa mingsinggipa poedorangoni ja-manchalga;

ba nikgualengnaba donga; indiba anga gipin poedorango mandeko 
chattaming, namgipa milgipa matchuming toaniko poraiman-kuja. 
Depanteko, kawatangko bolrangming toaniko pil nipil nika. 
Chasronggipa mangrakgipa bolona aro bolsal- mine. toon, ia gun 
donggipa mande mikron mikkangona nabaa. Biiak nama, kambe 
dotdima inon bijimgipa, bite bang-e nangna ka-dongchaksogipa bolko

ka-dongchaksoa; indaken kambe pe-o uamang ka-a chakmanja. Pe-a

A-chikrang an-tangtangko mikka stiloniko chel-chakna re-jakko 
jakkala; ja-manosa sattiko ba chattako jakkalbaa. An-tangtangko 
chel-chakgipako re-jakming sattiming toaniara mairongpile krabea. 
Chrako anedeko salakim aro donaandalaipa ineba agana. Sal ding-ao 
ja-reon, an-ching salakimko aro salni teng-aniko dongandalataniko 
am-a. Indake a-sel ong-o namgija obosta soko, chraan depantean

naljokaniko aro chel-chakaniko on-a. Nomil mikgilko te-je ina; 
ku-chilko gitchakrairai so-ome name nitoana rangre dona. 
Mangrakgipa kimkimgipa mandeko chini rong-ma ming toaniko 
nika. Chini ro-ong dal-gipa a-gani ro-ongna bate chi-jengjeng nitobata 
aro chi ta-rakomangba ua kimkim dongaia. Chi dal-bao katsrapgijade 
rong-mao gae chakrikna man-a. Indakgipamingsa Kabeo mandeko 
toa. 
Chrako angdeko matming do-ming changni chang toanikoba nika. 
Matburingrangni gisepo, matchuan A-chikrangna jakkaltobatgipa 
ong-a. Aalo matchu doka; a-grango jile samsi rate on-e nangani salona 
milatsamsoa. Grong chi-jangjang nitogipa simchi simchiiikgipa aro 
dal-a chu-soke ki-me salarenggipa matchuni mikkango, ma-a noa 
mangkrame mangrakgipa an-gisime chananggipa depanteko nikpila. 
Indaken, uni sio, “Angni grong, Angni simchi,” “A-grango ekilgipa” 
ine grapmanggasia. Ja-takkim jaktakkim dakana uko matma ia-chik 
ineba agana. 
Do-mara do-gepchi jatsa. Do-ma kattara ‘jojong’ inani pal,

depantena ka-sae jakkalgipa kattaba ong-a. Kabeo jojong inani pal 
Do-ma inaniara ma-ani ka saani ning-tuaniko parakani ong a. A-gani

ma-gipa depanteni sio uko do-ma grana aipokming toe sintea. 
SQ-atoln ba sarao gitok de-dange ro-e gisikgipa chananggipa 
do-bipamingba chrako depanteko toa. 
Detangni kawatangni ba ma ani sio bon o, salniangengjok ine

Janggi tanganio samtangtangni nitoaniko nike nitogipa kragipa

Indakesa salniangako janape, sigipako bon-gipako chamesaa. Sal napo

“Mitena Rambudemana bijak name pinika, 
Katchina Rekon Pantena kambe name agana; 
Indakesa mite pe-a apako, 
Katchi wang-a do-mako.” 
AGANCHQTANI 
Dewansing Rongmuthu A-chik Katta Agananirangko aro 
ring-anirangko chue ra-a aro uarangko namsranggipa poedo ine 
de-doe agana. A-chik A-songni depanterango ning-in ning-in ba 
dongnue donggipa, dingtangmanchagipa be-en bimangni, gisik 
ka-tongni gunrang donga. Indiba uamangni jattang a-songtangna 
ka*saani, ka-dongani, miterangni gadango ong-pilgipa, mite mande 
ong ani aro muni man-ani gunrangko basakobade badepil-e 
uamangna on-a ine Rongmuthu agana. 
Sianggimin bon-anggimin A-chik depanterang demechikrang, 
ka dongani aro nitoani chanangani gunrang ong e dongaia aro 
uamangni bidingo maming namgijako kragijako janapani dongja aro 
ring-gipani namnika gitasa sigiminko mesokaia ine, Rongmuthu uni 
“The Epic Lore of the Garos” ki-tapo Aganchenganio agana. Kabe 
ring-anioba sigipa bon-gipani gunrangko de-doe agana; simtekani 
donggenchimoba jripaia. 
Chasong gitalni bi-sarangde an-tangtangni jatni man-rikani 
giminan uiangtokjaengjok. Grapmangtata, grap me-chi sola aro sinti 
do-pilgimani rokom bewal aro indakao jakkalgipa knatogipa 
me-sugipa kattarangko jakwatangtokengjok. Kabe ring-ani bang-a 
songrangon gimaangengjok. Songsarekrangsa grapmangtataniko, 
Kabe ring-aniko dakachim. Da-o bang-bataan Christian toromko ra-e, 
Kabe ring-anikode jegalangtokengjok. Agat A-Sangmade, da-oba mite 
katchini bimingko minggija Kabe ring-oba namaigenchimba ina.

Englishode, nama namgijako basegija gimikko galatgopaniko, 
“bi-sa auatani chiming bi-sako galchapata” ine agana. Jatni dakbewal 
rikbewalrangoniko badiako ra-na badiako galna tik ka-chongmotna 
nanga. A-chikrangni gipin rokomni ring-anirang jatni manianirango 
aro ring-susaanirango ring-anichi tangbangangkuna ka-dongna 
man-a. Kabede indaka cholko man-paja. Kabe ring-na 
changgiparangba sie bon-angtokengjok. Mikkangchi sal sokbagen, 
jeon an-ching “Kabe”-ko pujue done, unan Kabe ring-na 
grapmangtatna sinti do-pilgimna nanggen.

DAKMESOKANI 
‘Drama’-ko Achikkuo ‘Dakmesokani’ ine dona. “Drama” kattara 
Greek katta ‘dram’-oni ongkata. ‘Dram’ ni ortoara ‘dakna-kana’ ba 
‘dakmesokna’. Indake mande jinmana dake-ka e ba dakmesoke 
mesokanikon ‘Drama’ ine agana. Dramako dalgipa bak (Act) aro uni 
ning o chonkalgipa bakrang (scenes) ba mesokanirang dake suala. 
Act- ko Chap DilmOilg aro Scene- ko Mesokani ineba jakkala. 
Dakmesokanirangko mande jinmana dake-ka-e mesokna sea. 
Dakmesokanio on-gipa obostarang aro daka-kaanirang mandetangko 
kusi ong-ata aro gisik balsrangata. 
Dakmesokania dakmikani ong-a; uno ong-chongmotani grianio 
bebera-ani donga. Bi-sa kal-e roanio ong-chongmotaniko dokpindape 
done, uan ong-chongmotani ine bebera-a. Dokta kantongkon gari ine 
salgimik salrurue bi-sa kusi ong-na man-aia. Dal-giminrangba 
ong-chongmotani ong-ja ine uieba, Dakmesokanio nikenggipakon 
ong-chongmotani gita ra-e, nimitingo ka-dinga ba grapa. 
Dakmesokchakramo nikenggipa, uni mikkango dongenggipa ba 
ong-enggipakon kakket ine bebera-e gisik an-senga. 
Nienggiparangni Bakrimpaani 
Dakmesokani chu-sokgipa ong-na gitade, nienggiparangni 
chu-gimik bakrimpaaniko nanga. Nienggiparang Dakmesokanio 
ong-enggipa obostarangko gisiktangtango ong-chongmotani gita ra-na 
nanggen. Dakmesokanisan ine uioba, Bak Ra-enggiparangni kusi 
ong-anio kusi ong-pae ka-ding an-sengna aro uamangni dukni somoeo 
mikchi u-sitpana man-osa bakrimchong-motani donga.

Dakmesokanio gisik ka-tong gimikchi bak ra-pae, uamang gisik 
an-senga; uamang dikdiksanade an-tangni, nokdangni aro songsarni 
janggi tangchongmotenganio neng-nikanirangko guala. Badiaba 
Dakmesokanirango jatni songsarni bidingo jeraani dongon, uamang 
jeraanio bak ra-paa, maikoha jegala aro sanaba chakpaa. 
Nigiparang Dakmesokaniko ba uni badiaba bakko jegalode, 
Dakmesokaniko segipa, badiaba bakko, ritingrangko, kattarangko aro 
obostarangkomangba setaipilna ba namdapatna draatako man-a. 
Dakmesokaniko namnikjaode, hohoe, uko mesokaniko dondikatna 
man-a. Indake Nigiparanga Dakmesokanini chu-sokani kosako 
183 
bilmanbea. Uni giminsa Nigiparangko Dakmesokanini mongsonggipa 
Bakrango man-chapata. 
Dakmesokanini Ro*a Ro*gijani 
Dakmesokaniko segipa Dakmesokanini ro-a ro-gijanina 
simsakbena nanga. Dakmesokani kan-dikdugaode, Nigiparang 
an-tangtangni tikit bregipa korosnan tulija ine tangka dabipilnaba 
donga. Ro-dugaora kam a-sel bon-a ine chanchie Dakmesokanina gisik 
chu-gimik on-ja. Un baksanaba aratnikpile ajamtokskanaba donga. 
Janggi Tanganiko/ Ong-chongmotaniko Daksningani 
Badiaba Dakmesokaniranga ka-ding-an-sengatani; 
badiabarangara dukni; badiabarangora kusi ong-aniba dukniba brina. 
Mandeni janggi tangani indaken ong-a. ‘Dakmesokaniara janggi 
tanganiko daksningpaani ong-a, ine Greek philosopher Aristotle 
agana. Dakmcsokanio daka ka-anirang dake-ka-e nikgiminmanchao 
pangchakatgipa ong-janaba donga; uarang Dakmesokaniko 
senokgipani a-salarigipa gisikonisa wegadonaba donga. Indiba 
bang-bataon janggi tangchongmotani obostarangkon Dakmesokanio 
mesokpila. Bak Ra-giparang ua-an angan ba salanti 
nikronggiparangan ong-engnaba gnang; maina Dakmesokaniko 
segipa nikronggiparangon Bak Ra-giparangni kam-cholonko 
pangchakatronga.

bang-en donga; uarangoni mongsonggipa bakrangara iarang ong-a:

(v) Dakmesokchakramo Nanggipa Bosturang :

Dakmesokchakramo nangchongmotgipa bosturangko on-aniode 
Dakmesokaniko segipa jakgitela. Mitam senokgiparang “symbolic” 
cholbewalko jakkala. Chong-motan nanggipa bosturang gimikko 
on-gija, C/ii'n -kosa on-aia. Jekai, dal-gipa office ge-sako mesokna 
skode, chokki table ge-prak donari uko office mingaia. Nigiparangba 
ukon office ine bebera-aia. 
(vi) Ninagipa Dol ; Dakmesokanio jinmani kamrangkon 
mesolqpila. Nikronggipa ku-agananikon Bak Ra-giparangba ku-agana; 
bewalrangko mesokan baksanaba saksa sakgipinming 
nangrimrimgrikani bewalkoba paraka. Indiba Nigiparangan 
dongjaode, Dakmesokaniko sawasa nigen? Dakmesokanini 
chu-sokanina Nigiparangkode nangtelaia.

GOLPO BIKROK (PLOT) 
Dakmesokanio Golponi Bikrok dongna nanga. Dakmesokaniko 
senasion, daka-kaanirang maidake regen, uni Bikrokko tarichengna 
nanga. Daka-ka ani maidake re-gen, uko tarisochengjaode, 
chong-motan golponi bimang-bikrok dongchengjaode, daka- 
ka-anirang maidake bano bonegen uijani gimin, Dakmesokani bil-si 
bQ-dak ong-a. 
Dakmesokaniko seenggipa, daka-ka-anirang aro obostarangni 
Bikrokko name sulsul tarichengna nanga. lako dakosa, badiako 
mongsonge ra-gen, bako niksengatgen, bako ritchenge dongen, 
pilakko uia. Uisoani gimin, mangsongsoaniona sokatna gita ‘maidake’ 
aro ‘maini gimin’ indake ong-a, uko Dakmesokanio mesokna man-a. 
Ian Bikroko donsogimin Bakrang, Bak ge-sa Bak ge-gipinrangming 
maidake nangrima, nangea aro nangtinggrika uko ma-sinaba altuata. 
Golponi Bikrok Bak Ra-giparango ba Kal-giparango pangchaka. 
Manderangni gisikni re-anirangni, skanirangni aro maikoba dakna- 
ka-na draatanirangni mingsa minggipinming nangrimrimani aro 
bilmangrikani kri golpo re-rikrika. Golponi Mongsonggipa Bikrok 
(main Plot) ge-sasan ong-bata. Adita nangbakrikrikgipa ba Bipek 
Bikrokrangba (sub-plots) dongna man-a. Kal-enggiparangni 
nangtinggrikanioba Golponi Bikrok ba Bikrokrang pangchaka. Bang-a 
Dakmesokanirango, Daka-Ka-anibimangara Bak Ra-enggiparangni,

uamangni gisikni bilakanirangoni aro bilgrianirangoni nakata. 
Bipek Bikrokrangara Dakmesokanini mongsongani komikalgipa 
bikrokrang ong-a. Bipek Bikrokrang Ja-pang Bikrokko 
mangrakdapata. Bipek Bikrok basakobade Ja-pang Bikrokni re-anioni 
bikpilskaa. Bipek Bikrokrangara Bak Ra-enggiparangni janggi 
tanganio nangtinggrikchongmotanirangko, chong-motan uamang 
saksa sakgipin baksa maidake nangegtika, meliama dingtanggrikama, 
uko name sandie nia. Keneth Mominni ‘‘Nokdang”-o Balkam aro 
Tonteni aroba Medik aro Jambalni gisepo nangtinggrikaniko mesoka. 
Bikpilgrikgipa manderangoni, skanirangoni aro gisik 
on-anirangoni nangtinggrikani ong-kata. tan nama aro namgijani 
gisepo, ka-donggipa namgipa mande aro namgijagipa denggugipa 
ka-pong mandeni gisepo ong-na man-a. landake nangtinggrikaniko 
“Kamni Bite’’-o Sirang aro uni ma-a noni gisepo nika. 
Jottonlca-draani ba tikkelaniara an-tangni donsoginim rasongoni, 
songsami ja-rikenggipa niam bewalrangoni, aro mande salsani gisiko 
neng-nikanirangoni, jajrenganirangoni ba jajaanirangoni ong-kata. 
Maiba tikkelani ba nangtinggrikanian Dakmesokani golponi 
bolgro ba dengdalchakani ong-a. Golponi Bikrok ia 
nangtinggrikanioni a-bachenga; unikode uko maidake namata, ua 
namatani baksa golpoba bon a. lani gisepgriko golponi mikkangchi 
re-roroani aro daka-ka-anini tang<ona tang-doa dalunirang dongkua. 
Mitapgipa daka-ka-anirang dongrikrika aro badiaba bakosa 
badiaba dolna name golpo bon-a. Bon-kamao namani namgijani 
kosako chea; basakobade ia bikpilskanaba man-aia. “Nokdang,” 
“Kamni Bite” aro gipin A-chikku Dakmesokanirango cheaniko nikja. 
SKANGSRANGNI (CLASSICAL) DAKMESOKANIRANG 
Skangsrangni Dakmesokanirango Dakmesokani Bimangko bak 
5 dake suala. Uarangko sulsul nina: 
1. Talatchengani (Exposition) 
Talatchenganio Katta Bichongko aro Bak Ra-enggiparangko 
agane talate on-chenga. Dakmesokanini golponi a-bachengna 
skangara mai ong-achim aro Bak Ra-enggiparang da-o ong-enggipa 
obostaona maidake sokbaaha, uko iano talate on-a. 
Talatchenganiko dalgaode, Nigiparang golponi miksonganiko aro

re*aniko talja ba ma-sija. Talatchengania, Dakmesokanio Bak 
Ra-enggiparangko, batanggimin obostarangko, manderangni 
nangbakgrikaniko aro uamangni kam-cholon baksana gisikni 
bewalrangko uina dakchaka. 
Talatchenganiko on-ani chol-bewalrangba gnang: 
(a) Aganchengani ba Prologue: 'Pro’-ni orto skang ,aro 
leigen’-ni orto ku-agana . Dakmesokaniko a-bachengna skang, adita 
rhingrangko jakkale, Aganchenganiko daka ba talatchenganiko on-a. 
lako Bak Ra-enggipatangoni saksa daka ba Ring-ra-Ring-sal dongode, 
ua talatchenga. 
188 
(b) Ku«aganaiiio Man>chapstani: Talatchengani dongjaode, 
Nienggiparangna uiatna nanggipa bakko, Dakmesokanini 
a-bachengachipakan, Bak Ra-giparangni ku*agaDgrikanio 
man-chapatskaa. 
(c) Ku*agaiuini Bewal : Ku aganani bewalchi, ba sul salachi 
uina nanggipa bakona Nienggipani gisik on-aniko salbaa. 
(d) Uina Skaniko Wachaatani: Nienggiparangni uina skaniko 
wachaata, jedakode uamang golpoko ma-sina gisik on-batskagen. 
2. Daka-Ka'anini Wa*sarikrikani (Rising Action) : 
Dakmesokaniko Bak 5 dakaoni gnigipa Bakode action ba daka-ka-ani 
tang-dojok ba wa-sabatjok. Maiba bikpilgrike chanchiani ba 
nangtinggrikaniko, protagonist-oba mongsongbatgipa Bak Ra-gipao 
nakatata. 
Dakmesokanio Bak Ra-enggiparangni daka-ka anirang 
Nienggiparangni gisikko ding-atroroa aro gisik neng-atrikrika. 
Daka-ka-anini wa-sarikrikani kri, Nienggipa sakprakan ‘mai 
ong enga’ -na gisik nangbatroroa aro uina sikbatroroa. 
3. Wa*sabatgipa, Gisik Nangatbatg;ipa Bak (Climax) 
Ian daka-ka-anini chubatgipa Bak. lanonide wa-sadapangani 
dongjajok; wa-saaoni ba gisik nangbeaoni an-pilanganisa ong-aijok. 
Kam-cholon bikpilgrikani aro nangtinggrikani baririkrike, 
bon-chotgipa daka-ka-aniko dakna-ka-na nangchongmotaniona 
sokatna ia Bakon taria. la Bako, maiba parakataniba dingtangmancha 
ma-siatani donga, jean mongsongbatgipa Bak Ra gipani kosako

bilsuen bilmana. 
4. Daka-Ka*anini Olgrokangani (Falling Action) 
la Bako, Daka-Ka-anini wa-saani komianga aro Nienggiparangni 
gisik neng-aniba olgrokanga. Golponi Bikrokni re-ani ta-rak ta-rak 
ong-jajok; ka-sinkalesa re-aijok. lanonin Daka-Ka-ani, 
Bon-kamachipakna mikkanganga.

Nangtinggrikaniko ba neng-nikaniko namatna ba ra-galna 
kimkim tik ka-e, ia Bakon namatani ba ra-galaniko ong-ata. Bak

obostarangkoba, Dakmesokani a-bachengaona bate ma-sibata.

BAK RA'GIPARANG (Characters) 
Bak Ra-giparang Dakmesokanini mongsonggipa Bak ong-a. 
(i) Golpo, a*sel ong-ani aro obosta mingantian Bak 
Ra-giparangming melina nanga. Uamangni namroroangani ba 
namroroanggijani somoerang donga. Bak Ra-giparangan mandeni 
kam-cholonni, be-en-gisikni bewalrangni bidik ong-a aro uamang 
uarangko Dakmesokanio mesokpila. 
(ii) Bak Ra-giparangni niksenganian Dakmesokaniko 
mingsingatna man-a. Shylock, Hamlet, Othello aro Macbeth gita 
Dikki, Sonatchi aro Kalsin, Serejing aro Wal-jan niksenggipa Bak 
Ra-giparang ong-ani a-sel, ua Dakmesokanirangba niksenggipa 
ong-paa. 
(iii) Daka-ka-arangna bate Bak Ra-giparangan mongsongbata. 
(iv) Bak Ra-giparangko banaianio ba tarianio, kan-dikatanian 
mongsonga. Bak Ra-gipao pilak namani ba namgijani gunrangko 
sikchimonge uko bilakata. 
(v) Dakmesokaniko seenggipa bang-gija Mesokanirangni (scenes) 
apchangketgipa bakon daka-ka-anini Miksongani (motive) aro Bak 
Ra-gipako nina nangan baksa, golponi re-rikrikanganinaba simsakna 
nanga. 
Mongsonggipa Bak Ra-giparang saksa ba sakgni ong-na man-a. 
Uamangoni mongsongbatgipade Challang (Protagonist /Hero) ong-a.

Uoni kam-cholono bikpilgipa saksa dongkua, jean namgijagipa (An- 
tagonist /Villain) ong-a. Un baksa chonchongipa Bak Ra-pagiparangba 
donga. Shakespeareni banaigipa Hamlet gitan, A-chikku 
Dakmesokanioba Dikki donga. Sakgnion ong-chongmotani aro maiba 
donnuani bil donga. Namgijagipa Bak Ra-gipa Shakespeareni 
Othelloko duko galongipa lago gitan, Dikldko ja*pegipa Balwa donga.
